## Abstract

The proliferation of IoT devices in intelligent buildings generates fine-grained occupant data that machine learning models absorb during training. Even after raw data is deleted, these models retain statistical traces that can be exploited through membership inference and model inversion attacks, creating a privacy liability that regulations such as GDPR Article 17 require building operators to address. Machine unlearning has emerged as the technical response, but existing methods were designed for centralized, static settings and do not account for the distributed architectures, resource-constrained edge devices, multi-tenant consent regimes, and continuous training pipelines that characterize intelligent building deployments. This survey examines machine unlearning at this intersection. It establishes the theoretical foundations and taxonomy of unlearning approaches, characterizes the operational constraints of intelligent building IoT systems, and evaluates existing methods against those constraints. No current approach satisfies the full constraint envelope: cloud-side methods ignore edge limitations, edge-side methods lack cross-tier coordination, and federated methods weaken erasure guarantees. Three interdependent research directions must advance jointly before responsible deployment can be achieved: scalable on-device erasure, verifiable guarantees through privacy-enhancing technologies, and regulatory-standardisation alignment across the building stack.

## 1. Introduction

The proliferation of Internet of Things (IoT) devices within intelligent buildings has led to an exponential increase in fine-grained data, including occupancy energy use, biometrics and movement patterns, which can be leveraged to optimize performance and enhance security . However, while this pervasive data collection enables advanced functionalities, they also preserve a detailed digital footprint of occupants’ activities even after raw data is deleted [(Aouedi et al., protection and model security [(Hoang et al., This capability is particularly vital for compliance with stringent data protection regulations such as the GDPR, which mandate the "right to be forgotten" and necessitate mechanisms for data removal without compromising model utility [(Xu et al., 2023)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3A7VbLbhRHFDUBpGyyz2NTUiIFpJmeh8f2wC6Yh0hAQgRkEsSipup2dzHVVU09xtNZOVJ%2BIp%2Fi34mU%2F8ip7nGPjQ1CirKLZHvs7lv3nnPuubf8%2B%2BnODUvrQM5wfchFSY%2Fl%2BrsDPuWT%2BexgmPP5wXB3MqbhfLo3H873J7mc7U0O9nfnuXWqUOYmvYs2UHP978pK0p5VvGHKcLkiF8gE3bCKKsT%2BRsyT8SqoFQ1YNDyGsn0sB8w6HNNKKBs9kzzwAautV6ZgTvmlZzZntVMrLhq2cJRQ%2BgGyiehUaNgqakOOL5RG8vSGG8lqckBYcSOISSocR1plTcZeWMaldOQ9C8hDTHkf06kKeZUhQNPEnUnVS%2B4ZVeQKkgy%2FciZQUAmuWSBRGvUuEgsWSDSJxAtkHciuQLUmoXIlWHBctbkSLbBSJvi%2FTv6ERLmOlNBZ0wWhRichMJbKM8ayLMPPf4e4drZSrZQ95Iw9vXzQUU4OBWyqkU6JVA%2FCXyKXoj9Kr9UfWZS7yJLjS6N9srlIOBFZphw94yvw1Zo3KTNTVW1d4CYwZzW0MOzpk164h8r5cKUwZGAHcOqN5KiIuvVEBxiUoRDQR0%2FuLCpjT%2FixZz6KMsmZpHkQna0JQF4anGW3Hry8PWCPKDlQs%2FtJhWddpvT2eV%2BE3Xp0%2F9nz2%2Bz1nTdbnve4hwjJAEiMBisZkeScaPnmTfKUPKfxlRS91bHjIwBvQfgIVHQzhqRo7YM1FwHA%2ByOJ%2BA81qK9VheDzr15PDt5kmxPbIvLYuuXpzhclX3g0UYTmxjdn3eqP%2Fr8JPnUTfP8Je6DmYJDsuVIS%2FkVpW9WOyiQh8ge%2BtsZWTcuVY4s3Hoc2ztlaAnNymVbGjs67hNFa%2BXDRSq1v6D0XMJ4806qPMhyj2HFtmW0ZpbbaWJQQowV3zmgfyUU51FIbw0CxKiG7mDlj95qLMrR6rxQdX0A%2FYMfoqkQulXcYpPIidg3HeoKFyRSh7DaARqnQbQRskugQ4%2BBXeBRZgBFGJJbHEB3wKNeNeLsy4fHWXlcYJyUmH%2FhCK18yFVr3GDzCCAQF%2Fwhed4ZtGGwKC0QfMGSh3IgmeZ0M1ed%2Bb8quNglMjTBoipV%2BrAKWF7YllxWvkzOwv0yKXWiqoBEUtdFxXOZF2q5odR6By5rg1GJjg5ZkNysYOz0EQi2ZIZK%2Bhd0bv%2BtU6weuM81FukdIYjFSMR1P94fjWZVSJ6O7x1jKpzvXi24D%2BBN0IJV%2FiG%2Fr5DjbnVJQQdP6a%2B5eqRW7dWidIa3T7sVWwQppbuseKDL8cbrzmTS8InzmOa%2BUbhavIhWINuJHRTLdGTtXBB1tguhXVUL2D4TJI26KLlAeWmzDD8ShFO%2FCileK2zLyLlIo6U93bgpp1fqryTibzff2xiPu1mqVTXfH82x8MN6fCIzb%2BtsyhNrfHW3eWleMylDp0TZsNYWZF%2BYEP705KVNTOaRaHs1253vz2Z3J%2Ft7bdqO14r%2BF%2BLvD8Xw4mVV1bN14pv%2B1Fr27rO0S%2FYYzBL10ev3LGSAvSqu585lASMxIxqxcjsiM2rTYJskwo41dh9tRGPZjOYTCQ1FyrSEQ%2BcLHRWjqpN%2BSO2xVTS%2Fw5zWEks1%2FUkaef0wB5xrEatQnea85tNGE65hk%2FAOTloM8whXl26dfqo1%2FBmd31PaCu8t%2B7pdcGrTDHo9MYD7HlfJfkv4H). Consequently, machine unlearning methods are designed to achieve this data excision either by retrain models from scratch on the reduced dataset or by incrementally update the existing models to forget specific information.

An intelligent building IoT system is characterized by resource-contrained edge devices, distributed deployments, high-frequency consent revocations, real-time availabiltiy requirements, and diverse data modalities. Current research does not fully address these challenges of machine unlearning on the context of intelligent buildings [(Zhang et al., 2024)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3ApVe%2FjxxFFh4jc5x00sXogrsKuJPv5Jmdn%2FuDBC8Y5D3WPrOzJ%2Ft8h3TVXa%2B7y9Pd1VRVz%2BxkhgRBRIxAIiZBIkDaCITgbyAgISQjAKIViO9V987uWl5L1q52Rv3j1Xvf%2B973XtW8edh5Jo2zupztqIPnJmPqbwzUZncYy353LCfUlTTqd1VE0fpoOF5PBlGam1h6Yw87l%2B5XMqVbdRGRjTZHibE61eXlolLJDZ1mOT7ewUyHFdqU7t3DzlMqiszBYedykhEbHL3w69uvdvCnckr80bWdz779AnexN9XRtdvv%2FZ9f0UIrnx1duz7ZfIDbU2E7T%2Brw57f5ZuVw%2BNePLubwtXufMKSVw%2FH71y7o8PNvTqe8bV4WT%2Bzwnx9%2FeuJw77lfT6f80vb3F%2BRw792%2FcVVWKW%2F94YIp7%2F1w60yVN37idxdwON2vuawrh%2BsfXBDh9PDDMyl3Qo0u4HD%2Fz19zWU8QJt895JDeqI2n5VPPTOs4E35ZkRMmEYoKWSonpCVBBxXFnpTwRqTWLIQuhc9IJLWv8Vo6ISyldR56T2CZqKyey3gp5ALrS3IOS2JL0lFP3DSWzJzsVaGklwKRcgoLC7kUJTVhIsJzqhpnZIGFSn%2BVkRiny1RIMaMlh3UkLXDHmcxzKlNibHNptamduLkrZFXluh0K4r%2Fjwes9sdeuIetEJucEsAZeEbeQcaZLEnWZw6LkOOzAGjwHK4BFpYxyCsnv7b%2F4SouUkkTHGgDzpdBFlVOBa%2FgTi4xKESO0t3Xs2R8gFQYZu56YglSNhQC%2BvLqKfTq4RkxEAgAELbgqhZw12XsrYa1W%2FoTAVEzJhwXAIGPP9gYejRVFnXsNXA3jldGl54oE4%2BCpy175ZU%2BQi1Ggoxe%2B%2BuVHlpFaGDs77PwxkxGygNvl5S%2B320uxj%2FWYxRnCBiIYg%2FEeSV9hev4ukJtZcCil51rVMg8sWmiOXIPVUmHmMmewqLMzJa51CUdFo6bEGmRe5sxNBYXxG5D3REWUeYotw2cF5Ix%2F4UxeN87BDVkZFOXacjQchbDHJDcVY%2BSuripjfSh%2BT9wwCwpCRiKOREE%2BM2gZmOtkKTI0SvDFLZTADVg3IeemYixttglR2nIrFOwqZKMhaW4HV0eOyQrSik2BLAuNUNs7AsF07Fqg3IxS29BpTCP3q56j2faNyCiveEHFJdOcN5MGJTH5roI6NWtaIX0X2iSoVipTBcU2uZ1is00TMJl1vCybSibaoqbO12qJgj2qDscEBQnMCZXGOm1PoHPzHHdrT9xhUE6nXAImC42k0Ea444FkNXcZZhVkvACvy4pb6XFhr0x3ptvB03aBcFrGN3dZogY2C8gDyrC0qkazSlz5197%2BHqyciDC9ggzroBefWTrFOlfaEfDUpSLbvoUOEsIA9CdDjjvHU6qpETEa0wkXZJBJL0pTdutSs8gb7azWBYBMMw83CY9omJAP0mwojQidd4pMlgukF8YpJ%2FFQ5kFmqDuy9ZpLcZw4mz0agzIACJAUwz9gQJMSCWIQnBEgkKHvWwpa8qHEDPo%2BmdeybIT5CFU27Q%2FgD0lyNVoZZRi%2Fx43McxLs8aTVzH6FHQ1mTVaKuiZJglCOIfZyGXMOpK5zMYb94aQ76BdY77nj7Q4QHHaeTmUN8NY9yJuJ%2Bgo%2Bxqr13niTtHM1XQIvHpjQXczay6ElkzlQFXS5WrnbXR1kNW%2B8exL7FPZxTBt0zG08eXaa5bK9fD3ndVZHNZa4d3i%2FL2XB9kkiC3BO9zJ2kKK%2FS7ouF1Qqa3IKJ4OzlvFtbJvBLp1mGrMlPc%2BSbpgTn%2FvZYyzVXbDfGOp7qAo23vON06mXqJlr7NVNaWfnmUZ36zb8Xb0gfW5 This survey will explore the current state-of-the-art in machine unlearning methodologies, particularly focusing on their applicability and challenges within the specialized context of IoT deployments in intelligent buildings.

## 2. Background on Machine Unlearning

Machine learning models tend to retain the influence of training samples through their learned parameters. As GDPR regulations mandate that users hold the right to be forgotten, organizations must implement mechanisms not only to delete their data but also to excise the residual influence of such information from the model internal representation.

Machine unlearning addresses the need to expunge or modify predictions generated by machine learning models, which is increasingly critical for privacy, security, and fairness considerations [(Xu et al., 2024)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3A7VhNjBxHFR6DDeYAQuJCCBKF%2BEvQzuz87u4kh6y9XseTrG3krFkrKwtqul53l7e6qlNVPTuDhNhEnC2hcElAEVw4cOCAxGFvHHOLuOVARGQhIYFy44BkCeWr7p3Z2XgdZ7W5RLK0s5rqfvXVe9%2F31auefnW%2F9sUkSgu9MxDj77ap2e72Wr36UicW9W5TrNRX2vFSfYnzKO634z41O4kyEffG7tfO3Ml5QteKbEhWtFu9ZmysTKQ%2Bm%2BUiviKTVOHjHQJlOUca7e7u1z4nhkMz3q%2BdjVMKAfdXe2%2F%2B5ne1Wk0oijFitR%2F%2FFKPIm%2Fz%2Bauu3P1%2FFgHal8On91Uuv%2FZthOLdw7TjAc%2Fe%2BHGYdB7j2z68GhEPAv4V7jwB87n%2BrbyFqCrj01H%2FDqAK8%2FPv9MJgCPp2%2BE4aPyvBrW394WIbPj%2F8%2Bn%2BGX%2Fnj3ryfO8Pv%2F%2Bv9hhs%2B%2FeynQOwW8%2BPYHYelTAF554xfzJQNw78SAR0oevPyXoyr%2F4%2F3TAb7w5H%2FC4FMEfO%2FbQYVDwPc%2FgQ8%2FDvDFt27NcwjAk4tyBHDjJ3ePGvu0GV5lbwdZp4Df%2B9avTp7hdy68e2ibqx88Ma%2Fy2p9fO2XJ1%2F50fT7DY1SmVwrjafKFNy8Mnbc88vf2fn2VR6nUxBRxq6VOWGYEKccyPmFSczEi60l7NWEZZXW0tJ8Rc6Sd9HJEC6zQvPBpeVksMGMxT8lImsIxwT1fYLlxAdVKt%2BOYiVlu5YhHEza0hJXJLQAtKqz0EzYqlCbLh1IBPNzhWrCcLBppxnVETFBiOWDROhts0zAuhCXnmAcOMelcEWZlBxUVelZTyh2jjGxCguErZxEWlBFXzFOUavlKQcwbZKIoCnWhWkuZGaHUnCIZy4iBL1lihbJQldTe3dt7AxzFqqCQndFVENaoOESOqXQs56gBdZuRFFQubrLcUhpIxAqej4022aSslmuuJg6TwBSqYs6oojwpsM4xhTXYFjGcJZRUwtBYOh%2BSnJ%2BHwmgMsecJ4TnyKfnHMtxPqy1rO6woCGsKnFwHyYVJY5lhvY%2FDohh8yQPPgLN6FlI7Ct1gFydHeSgpH0m4dz79BbYLYQXAZFwlIaSLikpzaRlsTDrxKWjFPSUz6auTtcEuFxYxNjMWNgUKkoQXicWFLyzykTZoHSgCQfB56bBjvBOAyXk%2BVNKlTPrSQBqXsAu8hIUinleenTA4FR4onN81WPyANcHz4KkZ9kd22vEuga8RBlKtY7vSp7ANSBYZz4M1TE46xA4VZeAIjJrCcjxsJExmObSOC%2BRltLdyeOCDsshqu2DnqToyVIJpIuHKtGfer5QqDcFVgw20oDHbBJGw%2B6xb3Jzx8yybXtuY1vUSNnQ97OhnSw%2FfCJ0rrD8kdtnYxHg0FDYA9LXNG9cv3VzbHFy%2FxjbZlXVmeS7B9jjn8AT2E0p9AP2pqxtPlxtaYaMBFZlyuwN5aKpiBmHKDeN5aDquiEAfLmTohAiPTAJTBlbYduv2AtMchoCOiuukCCEgNgJPYbHtNgKCimFaBuCq%2BzA3cR7Us%2B0O7jsJQHiel5avBAiTR9yWjZCHJoKMgnCFhUtHEHi7e7vBrphdQoNdKHlC3w4lGLilKv0Bw4Sq0WEcCp9bMwgdkYXGfGgKj94UGnZw5rTXls33UY2WR7gboue6bCBRTDTP0AAd%2BVAVMu%2Fdvrf3%2BvYy8j9VDwbNmSxZnjXhBrZVhA17%2F7l3Xn%2BvfAiGTXf2a19J%2BcGJNTn75IlOrMcH1vyB9YPHx9Ujj6vHp9Vn97RSaGKhVHEpmKzdbPfqrWYWoIPR7UDHZr92Lqk6gNuDAmH5y%2FgYK3qNlRaVe%2FLz5KVXN The core principle behind machine unlearning is to eliminate the influence of particular training data points from a trained model without having to retrain the model, effectively making the model behave as if those data points were never part of the training set. However, achieving this objective remains computationally demanding, as the unlearning process must maintain model accuracy and performance while ensuring that the “forgotten” information is thoroughly purged from the internal parameters [(Qureshi et al., 2025; Xu & Teng, ### 2.1 Motivations for Machine Unlearning

Motives for machine unlearning span across legal, security, and fairness dimensions. General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) introduces the “right to erasure” in Article 17, granting data subjects the right to request deletion of their personal data and, by extension, of any derived representations. On the security front, membership inference attacks have shown that an adversary can determine wether a specific data point was part of the training set. Model inversion attacks can reconstruct training samples from models outputs, which can be even critical on overfitted models [(Liu et al., 2022)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3ArVhLjBxHGZ5FdsLJnEBGQqJEILKVndmZ2cfsOod48CM79tqsYkeLZVmiurq6u7zdXe2q6tkZlIODuHDIAU7hEiE4IO5wWMGBC0I5BCQ4IiGUG1eiKCBLKN9f3fNaexNLk8PuTlf%2F9T%2B%2B%2F%2Fsfs28fN16MRVLmh4Nw9O122JWb7a2NZpv3NpobvV7QDHbwazvYDHvb0UawtdWLUy240%2Ba4sfKw4LG8XWaBNEF3J9JGxSo%2FkxVhtKviJMWPsxBT%2FobSuf3pceNLYRDo0XHjTJRIEnjy2v%2Ff%2FdXjRqMRpjJyTy5%2F63frv8CTcLp4cvnK39cYHuSRCl3y5PLdW%2B9fxuOc2fPfeA6N%2F%2FjvD6Yar%2F76Dwsadz%2F645Iar711lRyeahy8SJLLaLy%2B%2FwGFOfPxk2V9fP07b5KKmcb%2F%2FGtZjf%2F7Jz3Mov4KwbqMxt2%2FRPM%2B9v%2F9Z3r3eRovN7764zmNL3%2F8MzzUGge%2F%2FIjCnGi886e%2FLYvjjaO3FqK%2B6ZaN%2BubeWXJ4qvHgkMi0jMY99s4CH5dnz97HX1vwcdBYtgpvvf%2FeInvOkIFlNN5%2B75UvOOrvmd8udIrBuWXrev%2Blxah3P1la4zsfLkS9Z55ij3xUaifHZ39zXRumcut4LuQqi9VQ5swlkgUpF4dN2GRcCGktc9qfZzqU6SrjOW5F0khcq86YwFkgmTNc5TIkeSOFjnP1QxziZqii%2BoJlOmKcOW5i6arbHz7%2BuWWFkaESfiQwXbmh8qJ0MJ5wx7iRrLRQrWCcDsmSymOGEHLt2P319vqDFruWFcoowdN0vAo5piyziT4ifVAidJbpPB1XmkTKrVURpMlq5YqdRDIs01waHqTwX7NMEnI2UcVc5Nw5wGRb7G6CE4w6YFgYNeSOXMdzVimuNfqLCJKpiFmd%2BXAMC7nj7IJsxa1VWAECPPXQmRAww0dCBY5zPJPoRYpogsMUg2luWmw%2FldxKqICxSdpsaYZyzO53tnYeMPgF%2BIEEAE9kbpF0CGQZN2PC%2FTMibbFbPteDfAgJiqy%2FcK6m55U8DK73VllnffsB4yrzLILrM2993nXpkGV65%2B3ViZ%2BhB7ZMuVCh5VOpIweyCp07YIAkUySOYiHUnpGFFlskO%2BArEo6XYKnMpVPCIgFcJOAvA4bGA1tzYko%2BeBmXKoT%2FdaqckdxlMne4PaufAiZx5uODdu3GReVYKDMdG14kSiw6t6uPJNDznE24BV%2Bgao65lt5CMQUmxswoe8jkSFlUQiAFJ1j5SbMU1LOIGCorUk3hzDyuqphcPOn%2FKS5TcrdRcIOcRVyomrUoeP%2FySLmE5bI0OIcfR9ocAiCfxwyNyPeAQuoild4GNOCsdo%2F0ULBz7inUOM8kVMy6BOU%2BQiqIn0OelvSW%2FH9Gx%2FJErHy9v77Ve4BUAR2DOIgvIbGWGwph4iq78Hr%2Ftr34jLxXVJzwvDDaG0M0J4L1rlAkIJwpBZKKxt18VPJUufEEsRqLyOjMQ7AA4aQ7wVSLSYuOIJ%2B89sGZt6nTh2TjuHEu4QHUc%2BHGL%2FwVwVWps45F8gh5Ezz0oBhHTc4bzJ1MsYN70C70BxeZkyLJdarjsaedHBXSIPUCMdsjFaHiQLxUFzXFRcJz7PMxIzqOdV6lL%2BQKPTVV0aT6dKTLPMQZTx301R1K6NKApwArKTPk2mqhpBv7%2Fuldy33ggUQNUql4w3S5P2BHcI50o%2FshFwHSF1GlkB5UAApChqUg2ZQHmsooR%2BfkdupqyMdEGPDJR1dzBZ5m2pEQ%2BQKAYq3DuWJEPuAU%2Flg0BJH4O3CGF0VaTw0LS9QJ0Gx8neIt0biqyBI9pI4KuqnbkPNTp1d These type of attacks are even more dangerous in IoT settings where models usually proccess sensitive behavioral data, such as movement patterns, energy usage profiles, biometric signals. This leakage poses serious threats to occupant privacy. Furthermore, models that have absorbed biased, poisoned or erroneous data need to be corrected. Similarly, data poisoning attacks can inject samples during training to manipulate model behavior[(Goldblum et al., 2022)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3ApVfPbxxJFR5HCcsJBBeyIEQhLSJZzYynZzyemSBt4jjZxMReRWuvEjhR3VXdXZ7urk5VtWcmQkr4JYEEd04IcePOxSf2gITEgb%2BAwwpxQOKE9oLkA3yvumdihwzsj4Ot7varr9773ve%2BKv%2FgtPVaEqVVMd0T8zd40OfxYBB2xn0x7mxNhuPOmE8mndG2GAeDiRRDPkgyHXGnzWlr47jkiXynykNpRDAcb8faqEQVl%2FNSxPdVkmb4cRaByq9RurA%2FPW1dEmGo56ety3EqKeDs1hvf%2F%2Bb7rVZLZDLGW%2FvZ7%2Bktcro8uzWJ%2F%2F4cL3KmhEvPbr37i%2B98gNdzG3%2FpVYBf%2F9ofEfVKwNvx6NfnAZ99lSL%2FD%2BDNs%2BNn37sASEnVgPfe2iD0F4Bf%2BAgZ3jy7%2FCFBNIC9Nz%2B4tQK8f%2FXf9LIC%2FOKvPgHg%2BJdUZQP44b8ulPz59kcqeT3g3p%2F%2FSSmtAFt%2FoYQ%2FBeC3f%2FsPdiHDEVH6MQG%2FURJGDfjgx3%2Bjfq0Ar%2Fzu5Qzlk0o7ubjyw78%2B%2Fw17h5%2BoBAItEuYMF7Kj49iyULqZlAXjUVQZHi3azEo8KYcnXggmuOOsNOoEf7vBDrSQpmAZN4ns2Ihnkh3sM7uwTuaWWYc4yZwGWqokHlMUsYJms1RhQc5V4fBDiVRWmiV6l93XMywybeZSaSVLNM8AKmVOkKFk3DEthGWqoAisQ1SBQB03aWplNeF22V7BYh65Nsu1deztfSZkTLGWGZktmC6YUEZGjpKT1tIGOWrLWFUCSto2JRulLOIFVpxInmFXjH7uJ5zxUFeuzt7vfO27acUk0LIu6%2FeCSZvdk6qkClcf%2B73rXbbjeaHvS5YZT8AFclwl%2FwqaVgxSRxq6GC9LyY1PHdzIrLJEfoJfBdOVYVhiZOFYLl2qhe0yEsEu6llRgWXhouTWSsFmClGo6QUfEInf%2FSY7wiLa%2BTDVU6PYNarwOrOpnqEP1JRCeqJqMBZJQ4kjCZcpaTohpw1e7LpgkqiMvBJpPTUTxUJZoNnIuq%2Bu9lJqLm96A%2BJrlpic87zMACZAYi3ohikjmVW5gkJ9DamGPiINJmypC%2BFjNYsyiXSXIF12hAR0Cd6eVNL6BiuLNoA4NLgW4yp9X%2Bj%2FJg5oq4RKo52ONCRA9N%2BNYxUp6krdSUhUYYpW6JgwXvjWGo0ZwnbAUwILVLzwcL5%2BbOsZITU%2FqSBk2kZUnlEOaaElYK2aq0xxs2jK9Tu%2BtHzZZqwS0mEetGHXHp%2BTcj9os%2FsVvyjkNnugM0hMXVB3m4WgwaWgDtl4MvAY6bys6l6iTjkHy6TTLqzESO2nPZGFNLUxcSFUE9pkCKEgRZUkEBJPCkyzipaSZvgbhhgqcaYCk54fa1WI8WnW1GZRP1%2BIRY%2BXKNceXajPT2%2Blz3%2B4vuTZgqiaTRp7EL30iZwvWKEdKSOWvE4BOzcdRrnSQoXy7Oafpg%2F9NWCmzfS09bmUh0gIQYtLP9%2BxgIFBYJqwhfHyWVprYjBsAPSeC4%2FWTHihKWKnkZp3oibRHJKBiS3HrM6DqiZKyKArp%2BFl8FSoAvK1aGcE1iBdshrPDzR0EZtm2uAAOO%2FwFr2lo6SDpR1u4GPSeJ8sqGoaLFQo8VLDVdahcpcuWFohhtkK8SfK%2Bv2gBp%2BB3wwzk0GQ9IelnKAejDO1PeGFetoYBJJZmelJlZGWQgjfKWm%2F9VIBzexiY1VWGRIX3g104YzO6uNOJnQ21mnoGalF8hyLUpyeGtyhCt%2Bc1QzVRdJZ5Ssk%2BVNvSfuUme8fMkXfcO7ggigTXCCf1sQLZaMKhXG4CDY10OHqNKMpfqkcvwYkZFpRc8% ### 2.2 Definition and Principles of Machine Unlearning

Machine unlearning is formally defined as the process of updating a model’s parameters to remove the dependency on a subset of training data, aiming to produce a model indistinguishable from one trained from scratch on the modified dataset [(Xu et al., 2023)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3A7VXNbhxFEF5DEFy454BES3C017t2bJPcTBACkUgRCQrJrae7Zqbinu5J%2F%2Bx6OAUkHoJH8evwGJz4qmfjhJ%2FwAvjg9W5PddX3U1Xz89Xi%2FTZE7th%2FSC9LyDTd%2BuOhNj17Uo509Ow71eukdM5Rm0xWbdlSGiNpK4fkMwevtLeKNsFtEMA%2BBxwo8rpxcj%2BT6X1woZsUiildU6iofUcqtKrnrneTSsUYSqktTulxdGy0ZE778qBXgIC85Bx3KKlMGMaSKaoNJ0QhaCRUUZFM6DzLzX01kEUWpyzrzofEyCU4hxBpqZ5Rxk%2B5ZxgxnoC8Eo2cyKttT5H2lS2kwGaMvNFm2lcl6YYdZ3xFqkOwyT2ogEGWuIaEYRdEF7UPxPg1VB5KN6FklcJAc82WjUp6GB2lWjzt7kcagqjYxjAA3xAsuX0FGg6Hw86a4l%2Bbs1RPek6KBoow8S9aCxsb9darxNAEBTWUExUjpTwXaEKGtkZbGtiAPORhb0vKcXrNnbNI78NmxwMho7SCSAshluoc1CECyCCeB6iWBZIeGvGhJISHxtFQAQkLJPUFgOUZoIAJnKMWWqGURgbpLFjsARHtBVaCwfSiAXomgXOoJRvhgQ4KJaoLH7ZI3qG8DyrlYmcFtNdu%2BkmqCijpG0eXgDBCf9gnBzrWx%2FCOdOLZX0lLl5xq%2BTdyw0BXal9CEzRW20JMqHoB1ZLcSQZtHzkA5LkxIVpccuiWLUPoCiGVuCE5IeEqYhg95hIhHiBc0CTMDY2gjHxvla7W8stSBUDoNbw3mMBEGadTgtuiIrxKZRgASBRodMInPEQhBvVe15FGw8PMVCGCFCa3jAc5HFidAREpRKNIPfnEG2nQDdO26oNuBPhMXYjT769%2BQ327QY9peFQvOh44z2M8Q565V4KuYI2oBibJ%2BMsAzVTQREgtUmBeUkaa2gApgfes4r9Kgnl0ASc1omaZzXQEl7AVZn1k%2FXiAv%2B66MI7ouiIbA7LabYgXV4uPe91gAqDNzTa82YY32%2FBmG%2F4vt6HTdQWR%2FUpIHa2OTg9WxwPQZo2pi99inK8WH3S65D7E9Ar0Rgj4Nf6wUtany%2FWKKtA9ypwdlfP7D9X9uqkE7ONKPrVYk%2Bj%2B21%2BM14kfBKgY4tVij0eI971sxqvFezAxpvwIJ3s9TK3fbn%2Fu5FrkpuBG%2BhVh1mPy8L9t9cBuan4s1MEob7%2FBtNgYHC3%2BGWWe97uw%2Fglr%2FxIA3xVKz3sBVIPbB9T%2BR1b7vA%2B7tO1TDZ%2F4XZHNs10cP%2BoxcKN6vJxDDdt0tbhlbOCwXi3X6zsnh8enq%2BPTo5Xl1PhX%2BEz%2BF8bJCu6sVrw%2BOTk7ODu%2Bs%2B7DiOHDeF88vXN8dvf05OjuevWiOlIdfQFHjw9WMPUMnuDdlLDkZ1P3KrzLz85TCngf1E0nzfLGvd1bMU5dKk2eRuFyoSMmx9ET%2FNzDFQrtd1gEbx9TxtaaEOsM4%2B355XQ%2FFJ9vlxwhJFaHIfsUr%2BA0H%2Be5cS4%2Fef0O%2FuG61%2B%2Bp810HWan%2BkSnRXX7a5zyme4eHUGuJF8%2Fh3xT7Ew%3D%3D). Effectivelly, after applying unlearning to a model, it should behave as if the forgotten data was never seen. There are two primary research paradigms: a) Exact Unlearning and b) Approximate Unlearning.

### 2.2.1 Exact Unlearning

Exact unlearning methods achieve removal by structuring the training process so that deletion requests are handled through partial retraining rather than full retrain. The primary approach is SISA (Sharded, Isolated, Sliced, and Aggregated) technique [(Xu et al., 2024)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3ArVhNjBxHFV7%2FKZYiIYhycICgIo4UAjvj%2BdkfbyTiXa%2FX8YC9%2Ftm1bIgsqOmq7i5vd1enqnp2JgdkRyAu3OFkOHHgRsRlD0go4ggcOAQJEEScEJzCAVnyIfle9fz0TNZK1mvLa2%2F3VH313ve%2B973S3N%2BbeyYK4iLb6Yj%2BywvtdndpOezWgnbrbG2hGcgab%2FOVWnMhWA6W2suizdtRogPutNmbO3I355HcLNKuNKLVXGyH2qhIZcfTXISXVBQn%2BHEWC5Xfo3Rmf%2Fbi3txR0e3q%2Ft7c8TCWtOLRuYerD%2BbwRyQydI9W2dzN3%2BEpcDp%2FtPr6f0%2Bv4kHuKuHiR6sX3vmAHisnHzsg4OrSH%2Bijpwj48Oj3pwHZ4QDX9s7%2BogL4hX%2Bf%2BuDAgKfX%2FkJplYDn7%2F20yuH6u%2B8cMuX1tT9WI0TKxMAhAC8cO%2Fl0i3Lh968%2FXdls%2FMhVq%2Fzqr987eFHaP%2F4tPZWAb2xH9yqAzf9%2FRI%2BfArj6leXPU0yTCCdVvpRenU75X7TyUyN85sPplCeAV%2BSb1ZSXHnz1SXT4%2FoTDzezv1BojwOOrrYMDfuM%2FvpL7Ar7w8nMky4MBLj1757ERntp59uCy%2Beb%2FHlBa%2BwI%2B%2F%2FzfPkOVZwDPd79HddgX8OifXjp4hBeKe8TTvoBs8a%2BfSTaznTIpytXGc1VhP2nrVQA%2FvEQhHdZgJ4DXvnNuJsInsq8K4J4nbQR4%2BqVrh%2ByU6y%2F%2BpOqH6%2B%2FePzjgVITXf%2FDKjB8eMuXr%2F3jvaUy9CeCNc3emAf95yJRv%2FPxhdUit%2Feb9QxZl69ivqp2CMUorDxHhltiopnz6l0dmO0W%2BVWgnByeubMcym2ecZXKXpVrIhCnLnOEqk4KFRqfMBoa7IGa7ysXMxZIZmeJjlUXlOvpFcMfr7PyAyX6QFMJ%2FhpX0mjnNun6T7kkxP0QY78yNDqS1w88tUxkue6m%2F0zGJUIVAHCrz23x8dbbJVW8KI%2BaWWdmThidMGL7b5cGOrbOOo1wCneaF84A1niQDgDmZWYIQhaToaEUinWQ5NzzFLwbgNZ07laq3%2FUZErVJZC3DPLFI6ETEOt%2FXLqCzjmWAJN1GZtZXOgla8MzJRyAvpUArlRRZxTlE3z3ZjBYoTnOjAAX5Cya3qqkS5AT6UGeOB5wkZGWmdUYGTAjl2OnW2cXttfZvd3Ly8sXZjs7P5Btvo88CxIkskN2Wh%2BA5i4IjVSCbDUAVKZo4BhzsZDRAaz1g2w2udbcc4zsrAVyPHudhEmTKUyvQUJKNDlLw8rDY5LTa6iEqxbHW21qAj8Lqrzc48mEsSvYuadgcMXMdaWNYFXWLE0PQGT6HGe8N63Kjyrl89dXRona3V2dVKWCUHNyccQOlsK%2BaG1DnPOlYnw9puJaCD3tFZa1FkZFTq72sUy6vszfbSnUpEoISzSGZebjyHgDlKR4r4ZEzoCDs%2BUpVH0q%2B2eiQfHTnbUQiNyJAZ7yYonyN1oeplW%2FmGQQTUlrpwLCySZKZ4ku1I6F1IToR4LGpuzYTq4a2ne%2BpA6g3N0iJxCtLGMntX41WZA%2FTsLUBSuv6NT3oMAFmoTMhaLvEvbSq6tWHHUiho7KSQWYAeQPUIw5%2BY%2BwMQVskOsqNDhg0%2Fhhg7EqpC3eGPr7Nb1BieCB9ACTaPRWhzHoYQLraMQSxMDs8jPxpCQjiEp3cpfHZRRQVapFX6lKIOT5FMqYcxiRmZHCoS6sJ4jq2TORxH2gAN9ujcn0%2Bv%2BMs06WVv7nMx71KnBW5w%2FEtXkDmOZWNZDkNLOTkTF1Aw7MkhgRR5GfU2WCC7cujNeWiLFyg3vYaVgv2Uk5J0YYeCybUlUKPsjm%2BT3KgeDwasa4hyiRqinQtDrtIrEtKw9xg4VKnFXBrvv1QnISPDAYvUUULNuBCwAAgIOODG2oJ2pcOEKm5DfgzaoFaIm7olwIEqINeTQZypt0rftTIha%2BlJJFvaP7O5DBTcaUaWvq72laqGIJChJEoCh2aVc2RAQ4UE7o%2BGSxsJnXjHd7yvM51CHdR38OGBxSbw5NWmk6L0Fwjhk2lBbpLh2wYZlWWRfWV9z1b3Ia1ZFxh7BLVwzN0o12HDjTKispJrknQpONrUV5iEU9TOYo29nAQDxjCxZpG9C03R4Pkmn5yKHu2NqlLrqrCMAf0fFGXBlaFhIbPIxcNRR6OqtOM6u1gYsmiaLh4FMUKIEp7kqJuEMuUM8eYDjXt57SMcAsZwg90pG6PRvXpwD7A0eBT0E%2FC8FOxg6D2FdWgyFw9JExxTG9yOsGe6bH%2BR0FzjBpwaWzoczFVzkfKclKHhZ7QWFpyCIzCKpucY4hHZA0oN4wW9GUZydygDn2TZK2i7pIYIE%2BHNhywDTI6EP%2BXk9aQc8VJcIJG1Gq3FWrOREjQJ3XRwM9qbOxGVDmDvoQJ0%2FEX8aCMW62eb0vfkMemUw8XkfGdjY4NtG55ZLCnnZsY2qC%2F9ONS5CrzU1yc3JFDcwfUowRdsJJ2wB3Gk8mQ%2BDuLy%2BDs6RbfJGzyLJC6n6Flj3TW8eeFkGCd89Hs%2FGTODkH9I19gMcxT%2FhyFPVTLo3i5kBCay4FtKCqMTObfPolvDRfK7CheVxy0Ttygav1Cs6yx63Docxctl0W3FdVzwcmWgBL5TPBEIrfpfbDbqzWZj5YyTLlB1FGOh3m4vr7QWGuiKbnYP%2F9rsvmotLDdrrbOLtxPSyZovYEe4Zmux2VxcbC%2BtLC03W41me7mxEtMKjsLs3Fporywsriw2Fxbu%2BpL5it%2BlQ2oN%2FF1BPXwLjIp%2BxGfQb3YyOI4rYAl0wSER4R5ItxBov3zUGRV1I0OJJfS8AxFCroG8aZI%2Bi53L7WtnznDTV726NtEZfJ96ptVunK03lhtLzQij0g1yomqHGzh2IrfxeATCkDr8NqZ79bV0dJ3G2iRQmLPnB%2Bu6yNypL8MmQgmrCKS4hflny9eXh6qcHw3Ayc3sNbY1dlDKZD0GMIojraBoTmJeJf2vj4JHdXzojy%2FQxw%3D%3D), which partitions the dataset into multiple disjoint shards, each training a specific sub-model, then, at the inference step, it aggregates their predictions. When removing a data point, only the specific sub-model containing that the shard with that data-point requires retraining. This approach mitigates computational costs by limiting the scope of affected shards. However, this strategy still encounters scaling and applicability challenges in intelligent building IoT environments. Sharing reduces the model training set, which can degrade accuracy, for example, in datasets with relations between datapoints. Furthermore, the overhead associated with managing and updating numerous independent sub-models poses significant logistical hurdles for resource-constrained edge gateways typical of smart environments [(Xia et al., Additionally, exact methods share a limitation, they require the trainining procedure to be designed for unlearning from the start, making them unsuited to already deployed models without such architectural prerequisites.

### 2.2.2 Approximate Unlearning

Approximate unlearning provides a more flexible alternative by focusing on updating model weights to minimize the influence of specific data points. However, these methods do not guarantee the complete elimination of data influence, but instead aim to lower the residual influence to within a tolerable limits. Generally methods follow four steps: 1) calculate the influence of the data points that need to be unlearned; 2) modify the model’s parameters to reverse the contribution of the target data; 3) calibrate noise to prevent the unlearned data from being infered on the updated model; and 4) evaluate the updated model performance.

There are essentially four primary methods used in approximate unlearning: a) gradient-based, which apply corrective updates to the model parameters using gradient information on the target data; b) influence-function-based, which estimate the effect of removing a specific data point on model parameters without retraining; c) knowledge distillation, which trains a “student” model to replicate the behavior of the original model while excluding target data; d) parameter-refining techniques, which perform localized adjustments or modified fine-tuning on the model weights to erase target information without necessitating full retraining.

The choice between exact and approximate methods depends on the trade-off between guarantee of data removal, computational cost, and deployment flexibility. This becomes increasingly important on resource-constrained envionments, where exact methods demand storage and computational requirements that edge devices cannot meet.

Machine unlearning strategies have been developed and validated primarily in centralized scenarios and single models, often requiring very capable hardware[(Liu et al., 2024; Xia et al., However, intelligent building IoT systems present a fundamentally different operational context. Section 3 establishes the requirements of these systems and Section 4 the application of machine unlearning in the context of intelligent buildings.

## 3. IoT in Intelligent Buildings

Intelligent buildings function as social and technical systems in which machine learning inference functions continously over time-series sensor data streams[(Qolomany et al., 2019; Schmidt & Åhlund, The following subsections detail the architecture of these systems, data collection and processing, and machine learning models that consume this data.

### 3.1 IoT Architectures in Intelligent Buildings

Deployments of IoT devices in intelligent buildings follow a three-layer topology that organizes devices by their distance to the source physical environment. The perception layer aggregates all the sensors and actuators that interact directly with the building, such as occupancy detectors, environmental thermostats, smart meters, cameras, and wearable devices. Generally, these are tied to microcontrollers with limited local storage, and limited computational and energy budgets that contraint the feasability of running complex operations. Above this level, the edge layer aggregates upstream traffic, acting as a computational intermediary that mitigates the resource limitations of end-nodes by executing less demanding models, facilitating data reduction or running pre-processing operations. Finally, the cloud layer serves as the centralized hub, providing high-performance computing resources necessary for training complex global models, long-term data storage, cross-building analytics, and operator dashboards.

The communication component is essential to facilitate seamless integration in the heterogenous space of various IoT devices. The perception layer is populated by a diverse array of devices, manufactored by multiple vendors, which creates challenges for interoperability and data integration across the building’s sensing network. To address these integration hurdles, modern intelligent buildings are increasingly adopting decentralized edge computing strategies, where local nodes perform real-time processing and decision-making to alleviate the latency associated with cloud-centric architectures [(Salimi & Hammad, 2018)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3ApVdNiBxFFJ6IiiDeFAMiFigYZXt2ZnZ3fpKD%2B5MNWWJi2N0kB5Gkprq6u3aqu9qq6p2dKCTGS0DQQw4GTyIeDHgUYQ%2BKR9G7eBHUi3oSxYMQEL%2Bqnr9dYkzYw8x0Vb%2F63vfe%2B97rnrd2Kw%2FHLCmy3lq489xCt9GmUbMe0EazFszPNzsB7dRZUOONerfVpQusRmOpGLVK71YObeU05meKtMs1a8zNR0qLWGQPpnkYnRRxIvGxBnbCHxEqM%2B%2FtVh4Iu121s1t5MEq4M7j90t%2Fv%2Fv5hpVIJJY%2Fs7cVn33gTiwqzKr%2B9uFk03C3eF6FNbi9utG8uYjnl9%2FH7BfzmgltMAD9wlgcAPPfM23sAj1358f8BF099feOrCeBzl65cGgM2b%2F3kEEaA69ePO%2FwpwCfuzPA3d%2BpODJ%2B%2F9KVDn4T8kVseAPDFR09NM9xY%2BOLeGP76X4Azn31KpgFbT%2B0ryv0ynD3%2Fp0vvhOH7B8xhvXJkjw6PPb9Ph%2FcL2Nj8fg%2FD5mWXgQMAzt26fnUa8Ohgnw7vF3D%2Bn%2Faeohz95IAhN9d%2F2SObYwetcuvjR%2FcwnH%2FnXhj%2BxZ50%2Br8TYOeI2MNw7gdXo2lA%2FnqhLB88dGMz4SSlIiOM5rQrpLADoiJisb2mNklONQ1FnBIiDBGZ5bHGAMxiYnhm8DtDmErTIhPlXPTLvLDDBc1CbGRWK0lerXfmX6uStQzYwBoBzxBOWULMwFiekoTCizVE9TMyhHLO3JXKeGZnSD8RMGcUhMeOuXfk2GnKLOkLmxCFCPQQ1sClVkWcEI5b2GZSFY7ZCF9psrSysnp2c%2FU4Ob105tzGyvra2U3Cw5hPWSGCBUSwMt6g1ufJmyFn4ORSRjVLhOXMFpq7rIH4KKOpMpZozhAJsYOc42bkM6n0gITUUpJrxbhxma2SVYc72fBBJ1zmRG3jOaVSTiRiz9jAh18GLIwpAGsTcPMmkVYpKcrzcKupFJc5YvcZ2BtcE8GdBB6fGZU%2FgExEnsN%2BlEjPwUVRsjbeNZJeuCdpuXImXZc3X3eJw11q8K1Q0lAYq0W3sFiHnAkDmQQp7fl4T6AMIjOWegqUmJRqS7qFkKFjOObi6%2Bsy7TyF3KXa5V5kTHN4woXnTxRjRQ6wAYFmywoRk1MP7lkzpR2yRALDrQJBuWNjf8OQqwQtAtRxvUYGiBYFptIoEPFx%2Bpj5jg1o392aGA7LUcgy6pBDxhwsFVqvB2i4zaAKYyh0UHJJvUYUMhZFXLvFfmKkOyDE%2Btw57SsfUAFDkPHtlSI1riYAYL7Gzk%2FONV5vUpfjkSpHwL45aRiKsnndrVir%2FhC%2F7HwUEafIkrYiEkxQiTOWS7whOeGQI0trL0CyPBTwuO36ksqBFcyURSsrunx6w%2FjAEcL%2BmAyXUSA51ZlvMJTJ%2By4dWF9armHnihnSvAwLBUhoFgPPeRmWfzqqydhysvFUltaG0ynOEC%2Bojp32fZ3QpqaQ3uPK8unR3EE708KqTKWqME43vtDDPPKM63hwtwyXc8rNt1Ka1vx89aZrQhywBBS2uaySsK90b7fymERBoQkeHod840atsRDU2qnTGOY112tZpPA8iEEoQe9dlSKFuO0JfKDrdnWuxa2wktvVkpfzvTySZLStZJHyw5%2FnY7yXx6%2FDwj0o1l1G8dxBnbWxZ7Fz%2BLtE0uHlH9L3t2tl%2BL7mnk8ZBIffKKKpkINoA5MmFTxGbjO%2BkUD0IcYBr9zB9CRNUxqWpuFSKrLSEo2Cl%2B6HWajEztP1WrVeqzdnt6o88%2BmsNmr1NvaqtVoLY6WbXcW3ya6J2lyrHbRa7baot1uNoFlvtVlK461Gu9Oem5tvNltS5Txb8sldC2290 This architecture has consequences for machine unlearning. Model updates must be distributed across these layers to ensure privacy compliance without overwhelming the constrained perception layer. A reference in the cloud, a localized version on edge, and a subset of features cached at the perception layer mean that a deletion request must be propagated across the entire hierarchy in order to avoid partial unlearn states, in which some layers have forgotten the information while others retain residual traces, thereby violating data erasure mandates.

### 3.2 Data Management and Privacy Challenges in IoT for Intelligent Buildings

Intelligent systems produce four categories of sensitive data: occupancy patterns, energy usage, environment signals, and derived inferences. These data streams qualify as personal data under GDPR once tied to an identifiable occupant.

The regulatory regimes has three components: a) GDPR Art. 5 establishes data minimization, purpose limitation, and storage limitation[(Stalla-Bourdillon et al., US2UW02aq2V%2BmJpuPInul5bqFcWmgstGq4sYxyyUteNZmOp0lxeWpwYs6T1Wq2%2BVK8ttpYateXl5uJKa7lWnrM0VpaazeVao3ZoztKo1FYq9ZWj5ywZA8bOVpAz27h8Yb59rMFKo7aCgeFyrTUerJza5WlkqHBLUaUpS%2Fnx0QOXM79x9MSl6oC9OO222n6j9mViWvIYD0vmjhyWHBWnnwI%3D). Consent in multi-tenant buildings must be granted per-tenant and per-purpose, updated on policy revision, and revoked on move-out or objection. Shared spaces and overlapping sensor coverage further complicate this process, one occupant’s revocation cannot sever their contribution from the data captured by a sensor.

The consequence of this is high-throughput, multi-source revocations rather than the rare deletions. In shared spaces, data records routinely overlap among multiple people. This entanglement makes it nearly impossible to cleanly delete a single user’s data without affecting others. As previously stated, deleting raw data is insufficient and models trained on that data must also be unlearned. This becomes especially difficult because the paths linking raw data to these derived models are rarely tracked.

### 3.3 Role of Machine Learning in Intelligent Buildings

Machine learning provides most operational decisions in an intelligent building. It is the core engine for predictive control and context-aware optimization, enabling systems to dynamically adjust parameters based on evolving behavioral patterns and environmental conditions[(Djenouri et al., 2019; Hasan & Roy, 2021; Himeur et al., Machine learning has a highly fractured architecture landscape; there is no single way that data is processed. For time-series and sequence models, such as LSTM or Transformers, data is processed in continuous streams[(Dief et al., 2023)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3ApVjPbxxJFXZ2E2AvcEOwCFSsxJJInvHMeBzbOSx2bIedkDj%2BuT%2BEIlHTVT1dcXVXb1W1ZxpxCAgkJDYs4sgBkDhw4BAEJ9%2BQkDjCDQQn9g8gAg5Iu0Ervlc9Y%2Fc4NtqVD%2Fa4Z6pefd973%2Ftejb99NHNloE3EvbFHM5ce5HwgN4u0L220ND8fG6sGKruc5iJ%2BVQ0SjR%2FvsE6FLcpk7kdHM8%2BJft%2BMjmYux4mkBU%2B%2F8t6b8cOZmRmhZeyfrsy%2B871v4CnyJn%2B6slke0oMcKuGTpys777zwdzzWzn3%2BIwa89%2FLvf14P%2BPYr9HiRgH%2F53Eo94KNf%2FO5iAbdudqcC%2FvAJu2DAn9DfNYQLtPIiAf%2F1sakcPvrbBSlv9zpTVX704gUpb%2F%2Fsr3XKNz94g1L60QKu%2FfG%2FFKMKuP%2FrzxOkScDFH3%2BfPrsAwtf%2B%2Bec6wo133z1VlMtnBXz%2FT%2F%2BhEGcFfOk3f6gHvJE%2BPoXwzIDvvdmnUo4Ddvd%2BS%2B1VBfyS%2FQdBmgTc%2FviTU613VsCVTz%2F%2BIqX6LIRX5x%2FXA%2B68XXwYhO9vf%2Bu8gJ0nv6p3ykv83xek3L3z03qVt1%2F4wSlhf4iAUwgXfrk0bV%2BrpxHKtwrjZXnlg71EORYXDi7JVBbpQkjHnNRxg3svM3JPxjPBImucq72XyijhmXKpY1%2FvtO8zp1KluWXeMG955mDKqbRulqk0t%2BZQZQN2Z3fv7pcd432llS9pZcRzX1jJIpPmWo6Y4J4zIXOZCZlFSmK7dLmMFNe6BDyGEwYyLHPSuyZje4lkVrpCezphj06mYxi3UaK8jCbh%2ByoDr%2BojsKlBZM5bmQ18gsNSfkBhlGdcABrvaxmWs9wMpY0LzbAH53HdGBqrBeN5rtV4xoSVuZVCReG5yW6pAR3fYUrrAsdwDwzHGG%2BAUF54BqZG0LHAmR1K6x1ScyARkDJkkO6sMIVjh2BjKKO5cYpO4Pp4r8PBSLIgrpmXIxAgMCcLVUZsA9BZNkU%2FhMBrH8PyIMSJpCMJvFXgIwAeKp%2BwlDLcSCQXZ4kjllIg3JBbwTLpkZsD4KzS3QOeAajT2nG9J9E96nqq3GcI7KR8NRIstiZlfQNozJnCAig2U2UhCFN4SqzmJXiBkCiIR6woEWcWqHmecgCe3WYbWkbsZbYGlbKNbDDLXjO6ydrdWbZp8DrLbsm%2BLbgtWafV6d5gS%2FOtxlK3xbpNtrOxu39nb5etbq6z9d7u2v7ubu%2FeJmSLrnM4hKgArg78CDu1Aqd0HUoosxAlVRQEvAQ3w7hzVB1amUORlA4UiZmY3d0i%2BGCCj%2BQIH6oUmQyMUVFBbCS6zJQ42CO%2B8xCuRs64Lh3QILg85LqASEP4qqscM33PgQ5aDxbA0IrHDTg7hkvqrc6v1OIkdIzgdJBsmLiBgA1uoUo9wGXNJ3AN6qTQ79R%2FHFXW4KbicTdVPI4xxIAEjOOMTLQOazHWc9Qok8MpdfA%2BknpWlsJb1qAzwGgMObY8laTZqtcVDiRa0qN5UIVJF5H%2F0PZYSXQ%2BYp2PnkE%2FSaagcYis22w32XqVMbaKPFNRsL2zzISKYwn3AVRUq%2BFQNMhgkl0Gz5GsIKThaJJM4B%2F8GJirJiBM%2B2s7SFZAamw5y4aJihKGDTyiblZkZOTMxL7ow7Jg2p37TSaI9tHMpxLeJ3%2BKfHn5fg%2B9x9EDmWRacpuhuLMVporqSQeFAxjaVGs4aHBO5woJh0KtVVTArWHbw0Rm6HIOjQ8qecgMFSFL4%2FidVfWtGR2dVdVflBlPATbjoRsnOYdNkxv4kD86xwc1gYCY%2BF0KT9NB1DARjMiQMwcTh The consequence is that no clean snapshot exists from which to retrain. Online and continual regimes produce a non-stationary parameter trajectory where gradient updates from a given sample are intertwined with millions of others. The state of the model before that sample was seen is not recoverable[(Zhou et al., 2026)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3A7VVNbxxFEN1AEOKEuKDk1uKUSPF6v%2Bx4jTiYGCPLkbLiQ0a59U7XzJS3p7vT3bMfORn4BQj%2BzN75JfkTXHk9M94EBR84Ivmw9s50ddWr917V%2FrztfZhbzwWbj%2BlVbSNt7v%2F5wmg2JDJrIptaaqFJesOmEB4x7CmIyirSQUTbnonc20oYa95c%2FxaijGyN9BuhZJQiRE%2ByCmJVsiZkiJKbXM6z9WJh7EqTKqgvLkmwItTMN0IaYZfktbULUsKVZGyFD%2FL%2FsbuBorJgzXGDt6uSfILsPWVRb1pYuMoGeEwGyBLnXjpWOEXLhY2RjJB5JC8qNsDipJcVpefaATqFvnhRe2CRehMYGWL0PK9xIGKJ5139xIMUkSpnvdRvrn8PDhyAOJXYqygrpeFQHe9CWljz5vITUXJR7uWJWzLZ5h0YNmSsdUNnEJmsA3oGcorQpUhFbR2dzEhIJV1L%2B5eAq8RN%2FWWtDfldoRYwaN6Rojk9iVBK71ogUDCr%2FVLGOvFFRVPb5uiYhLYBV1AgZNIRXsnY0EpraFwRiA1kAkde4hDoXOSKXze4YA4OEPmHktAFAKBUTD5CMKgWs9OzC%2FFoRh515113p5QTmjnrUF%2FcCP%2F4CdiuDecMgdFTRSvrFy0c1dy50Ybe%2BktIbZF2bmMpFMNMIXUGQJ0kGvysEOUc2qC1syZZMclnlwmPhB2RskUWKpvyvFWKllbXTZ8N%2Fa22rxNjpgCjCKngg8r6TV9834rTFWRzBccmB3KFapiWOkvcw%2FUtGZ38K0ZBmYYSVoRzYZT0PXqMU0yYchglObrRcqdcgkMG%2FoNLvJ3XIRoKaLvhezfOxooowwJI57Y2Ks2uNMa2lkL0N2tkDklWMEM%2B0QfxFBpqEEC%2FhvwmaajnyV1ti4Ir5zHJ4R0hsAIStR1XycLkYWusCORqNJKB0gYKEFmBXIXCHq5J4Rglrck0QvzLggrtbIS%2BSpbY9j4tJdB4mcW7vXa31%2B722t1e%2B5%2FvNS0z%2BDuQOgW6YjQYHe4NxlW6Ab7In5vcbnsfFbKOpfXhWgMnlt8ZPtarg%2F5kRBxCTQ%2B%2BoMhR0%2FqrmbcZkUrJb8bh5OTkXDyzJsfqSfyioxOP9cVZ8s25iaQ1F%2BkoT%2Fao6MEjt0Pw3GYStba9e%2BxkQd9JU9C29wFu%2BxBnePPw1SelljffP9PpZrN%2BgPdXRCoD7%2BF%2FnsuK9Ua9LG0N0Zdk6LROQ%2Bqtpt77gdm30rZx%2BUu%2B4tsD5z91%2BdQFQbkmKmMVtr37mbK8fjgc9IeHg%2Bm%2BlJL7y8mAx5P%2BZDAcTRWHubnG32B%2B4dHwYLp3MJ5OeTR%2BOtkbTw6PSusIS5XWi8unw%2FHR4Gg6mhxcNYQ3el11eu0NJ5Wr55oDtnsr2b0G5Pr4JAQLmhsbwTytHmqZfJ7MmSS6RYsFDG9rn9GPXq8flzG6cLy%2Fj4b6WK37t%2FZUwNdx48DPPdQkm1%2BwUX%2Fh1wwuB2c640jq680zzE%2FsYR12plCXWE2hfdtZafbPLTcDHvJLOu72XXr93spLS7r7WYbhOtc%2F71yfR%2Bs4C9cq4fsc2%2Fu%2F9PU3). Reinforcement learning policies are harder still, as a sample's influence propagates through state-action visitations, undermining the linearization behind influence-function methods[(Xu et al., Graph neural network-based user models entangle each user's data with neighbors via message passing, so removing a node demands subgraph re-propagation rather than local deletion[(Li et al., 2025)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3A7VVNbxxFEF1DEEfEBZFbi0sSybvr9a4dNhISxglORBJFhCiCEz3dNTOd7emedPfsh8QhIP4T8i%2Fjyque3dghWOIayQfveqer61W996rm9%2FPBx6UPpjLuU3rd%2BUSbG3%2BdBdnWonOWZHDGVSKRqp23vtqIWkZRkPINCeNUIBkRYDfCNK0PSbok8ECRSDUJqZeEB77Mv34FSp1E8rgvgFn5lMjdEtLpfF4Fv2Iw5XE%2FuChk4TvE46gNZinVJodGUl0wacNpZUimNMpIi2ISWWsqwuWReK8DaZrI0K87oxYol6xpjJOpL5TKklSKnDK2pDin0DJJ4R3K4lSOugAUR2nlwyKK22dPn8Y7I%2FHQr2hJYV80PiZBaxMTw2lKFADAP9U2xaVqyiAb6hOV3lq%2FElIU0krUrkXLXSXj3TB2ReM1WZGCRC5XDWVVBaokn3Kc1KZq9kWg2NmMaxwyWakWuZUUOpVy3caB8Ka%2FV6AHIieQfdcbhCl8qL3XYB0cB9Kd0yzmpaIZr%2BG%2BhJJWdTZniyPxk4fQGjWA4NpEYWLsaF%2BsWDbf%2BggfCOeXaKNX5fm2LBJPZNty5hfvgnBTIPj5kxd3cjW76wCvvQZRESyhEZOEQ0VanPqm6Rw8MVTwWwDjPdKDgNAgbp%2BePYBU%2BBSNbCMMtg1%2FS0G2hgPVcZ%2F9EKiAQ5wsbDY%2FDBLgeNfzyRSy9XL80EJ9e5kl31LoeV4Z0MF6MJssLMBbfGXEXTkLYtbgwHXrHYpnK%2Bf4HU7hU90PU%2BO77TBt3dBbdDc%2BrmsKNIvz%2F9IMOj1YY9yiWWYwCqYBHJPhGK1ntORTzLQdwpxWZ4BIiOpBAhG6giE3omMNMANQQy0KlB6xGXAZs4DpwRmXVMNXAlDZe7wUOA1mDROLMc0T7LvQM5G13c9XMMV172Ygt1hJPS3gkpMiOyrD1X9P1UjzRJ0PPqtlAamkSter7HqVXa%2By61X24a0y%2BF7BhaTvY66rw4PDo%2BFk0qDBBL4oPMIMnA8%2BqWQHqBDfYAO02Hff488HfTSaHVK27ueE8bO0%2FuZZ8IpII3XeDAx%2BcnLyCHZzJTzCDYGzk4sd9OjSDiqX3nYNfXmrfVvAY68koM4He6aVFf0oXUXng49wO8T0DE9uPky1lbv%2Ff7N8M5iiw6X4JyI12x3fZSkbYzfFY0MVFHPFz0YHb2nwfgz9UjNMDque10bV0v%2Bv2LPOrA30uir2tEZQH6vvU7FNqoyO54MbSnuzvjk5GE2OD%2BZjKaUZLadzM7k7ms4OZhNtYuHe4DO6P8zh5Gg%2BPJrO5%2BZwenc2nM6Ov64xRE5CgsXL2exgPj2eTeZ3X2VxsravsrYHM5a37TClETPby7uX61zfO4nRQ5M8RbBfL55esgvZ96znFcItsMngSkUvgl1%2FW6fUxnvjsX8VR7kPvLfGxmlaj9q6HbMfxryvlaWx9itnvdTj3OR4ejyZH1dYMWnTgrk9lEK%2B%2FAGX%2F6Ykrd2ATasMRu%2B7zSnP9l4KtDWWfskvh%2Fx0sLXjVxdb7vSdLXexPcvkW6PiG82IX%2BA1add3dg1AkVz7laL8Aw%3D%3D). The foundational assumption of most unlearning methods, that is a clean baseline to compare against, fails across every dominant training regime in intelligent buildings.

## 4. Machine Unlearning in IoT on Intelligent Buildings

Existing approaches can be grouped by where in the building stack they operate: cloud-side, edge-side, and federated[(Yohannes et al., 2025)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3A7VZNcxtFEJVJUnCCK7nNjaRKliXHdmxucWKnVDghhU0FLlTNzvbutj07o8zMyhanwIEqfgo%2FwWf%2BGK9nZeGE6MzFB1ur2f58%2FfqNfrse3Kt84Jrd5%2FSu84kWD%2F48bXVISrtSGV87TjwnZfBBUbVdTDh1MWmX7EIV2mpnSKWGFLk5B%2B9acknbbISIhLdR%2BUp1odCOf9WJvcuxE5nGeetrNjDX5VwCiXMcqTOEY1cFHVPoTOoCSQhJMnWJgqMk3w87tiW7OqpHU3%2F4WDESuZVl60tYquhtJzkjDnUSG3aKU1SkAxpAI3V24XZmc%2FpcoZTgI6lilaLRQEEr6y8%2FarTyPs0Cu6QuG7akZsHPWXwEgjk5FghU8lIUB2RvdMECH%2Fo8BZyanS7gFzPqaCQqjX6Nb2eooJTaSo6JnUH16L4OOuH4X%2FiAb%2BxMo3SE99lQPZsO1d7LIQ7DnNjmAQ1zPAAhZekZKrB5oENFyYzU666l4DsEIhd9iGLddg6TSaRKnbSAFkjbzcQtgsn8%2BuPskqQ962WOkZAUAfKEG3LLxpWxvitXb3FoEA7B5V2XWg9aaWM66e1WcuEKsJ1rs8BEPEooO4BpYeXMQor6wLZPu%2Bp0CHuD6uxi2FcruBbUj0ZsACMokblbggVdlFfHBN5kiE9AESdHj45PHvek7JsUatlMN2N0RGJthUpLID5u91a32ik%2FA4SrGKCIAUslibgVweu%2BARAZDLnh8g0N%2ByJ8cU4mb2V%2BjTrAHDbgEB6RDJ2BleFSB%2FSn4iImapf5cAxefJJ0l5zAoX4Ncw23ZvAB2ZCBQGJh9XI2GfZIGB8nYN2D23tSVbGRFcC5WJGjUC8ANdZuBKpuFlpIviaveMiuVkAPIoAH1K9NwzQXyG6tw7JL3SXfLn3RElkoi6zFYd9Clpobjq%2BCCi2qLgDoIKtmuoiSRuoYGGGuQ4Tpt99qxjoC3Ohb9A6xdIkRkK7yftZ9g9gkqCko1sCbXC0JrMdBBrjqspqVDGr2lF0%2FklF56cPF9eBLKAaEUJt0J8534nwnznfifCfO%2F784W6wESTEv0GO9Pd7e3Zxst%2BCMGFOYuspfDx7UaLpBQ%2B8tNAwKfow%2FH8q90fiAOMaO7lPiZMlOj46O1GtKIvnVHLrY0tffzFbhTsBtOF4PNniG6fygUfv14DOuOMT0BicPN6aNhST3z2%2BseAYuAHmIf8CydLoVj6rSLdtF87MHSxxFqsFVR6cEBMrgLQ3%2Ba3x%2BSIEuKJEuluYnEFG9zrx5Zv07jYfeuHrl9UKX66zL70GppeWhBqDtOsv6ZYc7amX8yjdgyzrj6gUVhW56W37191%2BQUO2oNzdcxuvBfVN6vno4GY8mk%2FHBVoubayRzHD3Z3Xmyf7AHuhXuPf5H9zuP9w%2FGm%2FvjnR2e7O7ub052935q%2FIzAQrq6eLuzM376dH97b3%2FvPE81k%2BI8k2K8vbk9xs4VliNI3PNiI1d7NZnisuYEsRO1OLKgXehvXJC1%2F%2BqxF1EdOdCWoM517Iq0mEmjF0thOcPXDbCVfPUdu%2FL2MeEOtAvYWvxEoPJw8dx3Lt1LgSpMVHb6LegW8%2BlXPRGvfjnzWZxuX39SzqlICKn%2B98enb%2Fofs0Y%2Fu5GL5x%2FIxWkWgVglP0NH70vp4guEtFePm5Rm8dutLYxj5EO9tXYi%2FwA%3D).

Cloud-side methods rely on centralized datasets and ample compute. SISA-style sharding, gradient-based corrections, and knowledge distillation all assume infrequent deletion requests processed on powerful hardware [(Zhang et al., 2024)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3ApVjfa2RXHZ8taRURBUGoPtij7MNWdiYzmcwkWZAmu2lITDa7m0nJulTwzL3n3nt27r3n9pxzZzJQZKsF1wdBHwq%2B6aM%2FHooi0ghSbelb%2Fwel4oMgIvggGLR%2Bvufe%2BdVmttkGNpu5537P5%2Fvj8%2Fl%2Bz8m8clL5ROhFedrb8Y8vt1aXmqLdbFQb7dV2dXmlsVZdC%2BrNasML%2FJXVte5yfbkexsrjVumTyqX7GQ%2FFfp50he6utgKlZSjThSTzg20ZRjF%2BrIGZdDukSs1rJ5Un%2FG5XHZ9UFoJIkMHpc%2F%2F73i1WqVT8WAT2dP3Ll3%2BEh4pnVXa6vv325W%2FhQQykb6PT9c3Wmw%2FwOOX2ifMA%2FvDPY8CdH5g%2FzAC21y8G%2BPWjt%2BhhAvjuTz8acP2p12PKawz4zgRw9yufm035jxTw4wK%2BQZtKwL%2Fz2ZR%2F%2FfiA7D%2F%2FnJCy99tf0cMY8Dr7OKS8SIUvAG%2B%2BUplJefl9wr8A4P7uLWJhBHjj%2Ff%2Beg5QPA1JQBeCtz%2F9kJuXWx0t5CvBP%2F5hl%2Bd%2FnS3mXzM4CvP3Cl2Z1%2BCl6vAjg77YoxzHgymcvGOGdZ1JiaAzYfPN8pMwHfPW12ZQvDPi338%2Bk3Hid3l0A8GD%2FrzMpt3fON23mA%2F7m09Mpr7y78fgpX%2F7XGxPAzheWp1O%2BsX5yPmHPjbDzbdeH45Qr184xbR4J%2BJfvzrDcrl1Qh4fbv5xhuXXngymLl3JlxXDh1S0Z5lqwBmNdLUUQD5kvjKdlVxjGU6b6QvelGLBA80QMlO4xFbDOTmejxnZSZiOBjQJHYirT8Kp7Lo5IHjOfW842mTTMZLG0TKZWsQ4zEde%2BucpM7kWMccPee%2Fhz%2Bd7Dhy93Xt6U7Gvs7R%2FDsY%2FVX0yvbrIae55jh9vOsIaXBTp5TVmQa%2FzW0752mYmlJ%2BBL1kTtKjnqEeQuIHsTT%2BRqZh3gNbaBQoiMxYJryo1d2dx7liXKFzH59HDSW517VmC%2FSpkYh1ZjhyjC5t7ENs9QCdh1hyzU3M95jCojQk8LbgiaqpY6Xqi4Rcw1tg%2BC8Ipbhg3OJuMgoSqs0IYMaWnsh4PEnsgoc2ZwdQHVyCGALQtkKk1EjtwWqzkW8JRpBUeoDtFJGVm8MCzJYyuzeIKNWNgWMRoPS4Zzm%2BWWaWFgCpnAtafiWLhqIEuRZLEaOoec9ZWlT4nwIo44EqoWZ7E0lnIooFw60%2B6OiNGDw%2BtbcPJSLozzomVflMHy3KoE9y3P1dLdvaBXF1whL0ese3TVHGU3qjbcoIx%2BKdEcdBe4TklauBKZmQKPKm7GHgKtkqIBwAtCyWOumQcdANdY1H8ku67AtZGoHEdTkjeKoghjIGBEdXXanfHtNIW3VGk7UIDXSDiUgAJ0GbqCVtGtcFyIRUt0IoLTqsu7Ek0xJBuTCXjMs7EMrrpSaeHnXrHRUwmi4FW6zaKLPWVsoelAapAGQQMGdXdFR7DINaWkBtJGYDbCdILoiuQAMRPAODWDaVIWssYwcFCg0%2Bfe%2BdldmlM%2BjZmTymci3qVEPTtceGuj%2FOgCwYCJLEXRRVBKh8pa0HaF9PKsi2xg4MmXfUnN5uItZeR8a5GoPjIDCxlayWUpUwCRohCwI1alsUxRxZhbeoMoD8A916AX3RfxPlVYZcqgmglIJ%2BM8HU8LHoeovo0SKBH%2FmFFxXoCDL5Dk%2Bh5ceDKQXsF%2FoSdihSALzRFheZYpbV031Ni2GhDJrg8hxUTYSEGLMJfBkEVqUGCRUALAjOtdDgkwTTbjEQAyfZVCqYNIYoAlfAh%2FXUPFSi06i7SAsCRcbewwOJPelPADLnWKCeLKiFwsGhR In a single-tenant smart-home scenario, these methods can function: when a tenant vacates, the backend applies approximate gradient updates to remove their data's influence, but the approach yields no verifiable certificate of erasure, and edge replicas on the in-home hub remain unchanged until the next synchronization cycle [(Gao et al., 2024)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3A1VnNbxxJFXdgV8BlBQhpxYlSFAlbO9%2BesWciJ4Q4cdaLk13FzgdCi6jpqu6uTHXXbHX1fAghBbFCuXAAaSW4ceLK2SAEByQQSCAhuCIUiTN%2FQCSW36vu%2BYptshxisQfb0z2vXr33e%2B%2F96r3yd4%2FXPhUFcZ4O9sXk0narFYpuq1vd3Op2qu2uaFS7vWan2go3eX%2Bz3Wq3O%2B1Im4A7Y4%2FXLjwa8kjeyZO%2BtHwrNFZFKn0lGYrwTRXFGj8ug5TyC5RJs%2FeP1z4h%2Bn0zOV57JYwlCTy71vg9%2B83a2prQMsTTlz78Kz0FzgyfXet%2B8HN6kGMlXPzs2o0f%2F%2F2neFza9dXTFH7%2Bd298a6GwfvQhLSoU3rj7C7aksHH%2Fb6T%2Ff1N47fW%2F0NNcIT7PFTYv%2FPOjKHz9B%2Fu0au7yvxcK9%2Frhqst%2FeF6hfC83Tk5fee2W5ULJ1LEs7zvLA4KYJdLFRmQVptJA50KlEdvlWvUtd1IwLKkWaw6X1qznu7cONyrslkwRyFLuNLFbEGM8FWw%2FFWqkRM71WaL7XiPUZZK5WDIt%2BYiMEdxx1p8ubMY7%2Bj4w1spsaKAYb6JSaVZjZBv7xmbjXagYQV8kM8ZZUPpEm3EdIfVcnDBnGB8OrZmoBG54vXNN9GVfsjyFJTaFj6E1CTOQsWzIrVOBGnLIPX38ExarDAleVdiF5UOYLMkQeA9DWu8yIYfaTMkMuKBS3teSiTxJpvPdWFRAaSxtC4tEHrzAHmwAzJiCWsGHFARvIK3hfTPC6rGZRdfHQDkmlJWB0ws0oZWwRr3NDWcmXAnAkq8w2s7wT%2BXEsVCOmTV5ih2wikeRlZHHuMb2jE241tMKSSOmM0uG0qLwk4Xny8nIMxYarc04u8z%2B%2FAH7169%2Fxcbj8TrfYO6NJnv65Ed4cv71FXr60y%2Fx%2B4dMPX3y5On7P%2FPv%2F%2Fj9coGaL1D%2Biwpb72ywMaInK6tShCCCmJUwzA1bt3xcmQUG8CI2MnM%2BUwQMWo1GhcEY0sTiKVysAjUOl5Erfa55GsxgKxdU6ZkgW9p4hj%2FFikQLesQXjmeDin9NXiKCxgJIEimxh1fAzquaWx8Dy77E%2Bxy5xwXsh8W0JpMWZVGDL9Ajn33lt%2F8YeW4ZGzs4Xnst5v3Mx2P6uc%2FsSVH67o0mm9f3Dja8bplIG%2BEbfORIWDXiwbTKx9xSaWqNFKRqQxrOlxIkQkVJEYSVGmKPjEopL319kEIzRp7kmiVGSM3GqFaTI1diPktAhSqkXVG2RBE19naKTzJT1pcXSghhcFPkk2V7BxQacl4lQw3LU1cwQZnqO8qBHgI2SXSaXU4SfeVi7Nzwcr2ONKmNN2vGRvVmr9et3%2BYu9r9uH1wsxSdapYMzFvTq%2FtuLVxHK2JU5A4si4xxCs3736Prexk5dXUV6qpqsIcqn1h0B7uM9U2NB6khG%2FxIAyZk3Clguw7LgBJzCyMQCzxp7E%2FgiCyrn4HqZ8eQSrJM6ZAmfstQ4wkKmJo9i8mgeGUag%2BDrJCqeXFMgwBH%2BBzFNarFIQq8QvnzpwR4VKihJOiEClsY7w49mQ1rmYLxUGONJqYwZIYuScP1NynCkQ1wqFy11uZY3dW%2Bxe7FC0JpRQRYRyzS22D2KwnUxRsfAzpZQrY3bSfAoVknUpwF%2FOimgFJteCXIOHAcKKz6gJf9qlzqp%2BPovzyWOohjMWojCLCrnCxr4GkmGRI1gfSHzG0pcf8QInX4bhnEEWMPgAEZtRjRocDTvZy7TmPqzZUzv1jI4jnxmwxWcKigMcTXAVmYHvcfKwbJo5CZYHRhz0O82AKXA7zRXvxns58F8kRlmFRawRB1AXzl08%2BdQKTNIHqfvulkRHI Cloud-side methods address none of the constraints established in section 3: they ignore edge resource limits, assume a single model rather than heterogeneous replicas across tiers, and require a centralized dataset that federated deployments do not provide.

Edge-side methods operate directly on device-hosted models using gradient ascent on the forget set, neuron pruning, or local fine-tuning with a replay buffer[(Bellavista et al., 2021)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3A5VhNbBxJFR6HTRAs7HEVCQlKLEFEco9nembWdrIQO%2FZaMXGQyS7aG0N19%2BvusqurmqrqGQ9wMEFCQkgcQOKMuHDijhFI3HavHBEXtOLECQkhEBaIr7pn7JmsLYwsDuweHPVP1Vff%2B9733uvMt09aH87ivFKHu8nRZyKKk%2F56Pww6fI0Hfc57Ae9FYdCPY7xZXV%2FvDgaZ1DF32py0lg5KntGXqiIiw3upNiIT6oWiTNJHIssl%2FpzFKlFvEFrZH5y0biRRpI9OWi%2BkOfkFpw9Ov1d8rdVqJZJSd7rx6d%2F%2F3d%2FFTpenG%2BvJM4YbGovE5acbb%2B696N%2FNnXrjvwS896e7GwuAL%2F3keoCv6beP5wF3P%2BJvrwH4%2Bb%2Bq38wDPon%2FcD3AB998GddzDG9dU8ON46960eZCvhrgoU%2FlRYCbW%2BFCUh7982oa5l6ZiwAf3rq1GPLH%2FO11AN%2F53UKWHz%2B8WsiXAm59%2F%2BfzIX%2F5x1%2B4AuDGR3%2F2IR%2FIRYDbJ39ZsM3uDa%2F2fwJ88eOvzQP%2B8o%2F%2BrgHc%2BdXbHv3ch3ev4sN%2F%2FJp7GjPAX%2FzLy9YAPvrt3rxt%2Bq%2BEVzP2pRruPlOLPnyPsenrlXY0ufmjLamrJIhJOSPiQCs5YaXRMVkrVMa4SlhCVDJJ3Kj6iSGmNJNaZWQYZ9bvc0xRvcVNmNPMGS4Uy0EzSEVC0j8uNC7sfUZJRoAcCaxnMVeMx06MyB%2FLjROxKLkjhu0u9%2BtqYlyKb1ByzmFKkEUTRkdxzhV6a8YKcjyQBCxsR8ct6sbqoUrJY2I6ZYaPWcIdXwZ6ZXHiyG%2F0J435hGEPi8g5xFUaMeLxhGUVN1w5Ittmu%2BCaJMKD%2Bv1kIQSNWakRdyQQpJhGZBAxaNS4itxYm0MW8fgw0opYanTBKtXoxc2k5uMVUzb1gkJwLqUeex0LIjcTGI8CCWkUWBlkTxgqoE0tgg%2Fa8FoErYJG3EZwrwQZbKI2ezMXltnKjKhO8QiZsUhgAtmEhLz%2B6KoMnA5ACXIBcyQQIHTzkRTaOpzsE8Ji7e0SVfXkYnwEAB7Js7RZB4BApwFuAmT1sjxCM%2FB2lQE7qNsYoJLcLDPhWDMzIcYcXY9uiCdeqSoRPjA2Fi5HHLGHxUrkReF8n%2Fop85IAKrhpMoSHKQEAFBNmkW4wscszFIWwJXP8SCtdTPziS7jzEqR4DBss18pN95%2BpCbaxEeU8j6mCyBYsVW%2ByoCZSETM7sY6KqXsX1Z1ubhLnKWs5feP9OjXqMjYVRaVE8zXBKAWqlwdvFDyxu7ut4LflaeWxnKC8zkgRarMJoNTC6jq0hFJSFjnZ2nqDbWloXDp7j717%2FFPcFSVOb6ot14mWOvOivvvdH7Jt4a0K2iAJNhetu7%2BwiMsMOXZ58fxz2MDT596%2BjiS%2Blnyi77O9mfiztLXZ5rQisfYxfP2WNgkMiXD2c8Mtgfb2Qv5mEMts58wE21RKPfHVtMz2Z3oi0jk9X5%2FTc%2F9MqO1GKLa59YQ9xXVdaGynbj332D5H1Owh%2BKPNoCSW2V4Vc7y2cS6UaFTflL5tavZEG95mYae71mZwjjZ0%2BuCdl%2F9cjyDfQE5aL%2BU8gkZol5ObnzgPys6bEgXOXb2mlh4o9SH1hZcSxeHQ4mJegTVKjIpIo4qauoKVFNovCNW2922pAM9C2EYDj5TqGFvhbv821lISjvL9cJzD9SzlI10BpumpdVNDUdc9C5BlZdAsYeWp6xvYz4k2teuGynR04PHQOr21ARn7kpo2MnS6Skh3F7a81sh6bmDV0f9PRtZCZj4QIwuOen9OrYVU%2Fp9NrQXuH4CphcLxPqVkG1plYSccBN1O4dmhw These are lightweight enough to run on constrained hardware, partially addressing the resource limitation. However, they assume an isolated device with local access to the data to be forgotten. When a biased sensor stream is detected in an HVAC model post-deployment, local gradient ascent on the affected data window can patch the model, but utility drops non-uniformly. Comfort degrades for occupants whose data was unaffected by the bias [(Guo et al., 2020)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3A7VTLchtFFFVSULABtizvDlwly5IdP8KGMnYFXChVQKViYHfVfTXTdk%2F3pB%2BSh5VYsOdD%2BAGtWfIX%2FEY2OT2ynKRS%2FoFUtJBGt8%2Fcc%2B65Z%2BbP9eCjuQ%2BmMu4TeZF9ku7hfxeOgihxiTrhEIfUsOvwpWrjhCxqzriKvn463aHGa7GRal4IzUQcaVmI9a1oQl8SV7NTBZ1qoVYCiuimhPycauGEoyEtwGUs%2FnhH7DSxCaS806ZUeqofnp%2Be7VDsYpImjggK2drSMwrFlLURyDSJTMSNTeOd7YhjzA10pJoTzbKxurTSnHgDs1ZUwnlhjMmHcpmIqUwe2JL1qpc0pBwz6NCRlPUZaAkLCUNa1hKkn%2BzpdOsEl0pgOKVHdLYhec0L3dv%2B5g8QbjlwkoLXWUmkNpgFq644oCS4SNEUw96eQHFZ0kKgM4qLcAoLMK63t28Ys6phQa%2BOQ2lp%2B0m1tBxShsqWU%2Br7YxVeqdyySxtvUw2DWsa6MCNu14siU2guWgIuoPsuBE%2BmO0MM5WRJ2sQUzCy%2FBQAba1M1W7eg28O8sr6taWUdt5bR0qTa59TLdrJN0Y1CjJDRigKDpxgwk7QseSvAvhB9DrCpzFhq%2FaZGEhUW%2B%2FLbf%2F95McBHL324Xg8%2Bq3kGqazSh7R%2FSPv7k3bLCpZG0eeQXe2P9w93J5MGzqZCFy5g2HrwoOIM1hBX7d3JtEzsAw5Ny5X8AnpZDx6auQkx%2FYTKl%2Be15dvLC1vu60dHl7%2BA046bgp%2FPuTG2U99nLxVW5Mxv2f2%2F%2Bvv3WnTwVgbvYvVl4erB6hzT3AO7em5ivWTHGLYH69OQ70XLWS3brrNf832w2dTcYn42G4wyOq4HHyvtzXIyHk0mjw73Dg4eH50cTkb4PT6eHGPtM7fCd3Qr1XB1dTA%2BOHr86Phk%2F6TGi8CxlZvry9fFKyDzZiNX2Mh4d3y0O9mvYp6lri1irhFQo6w8w98HyLL4%2BY%2FG6TfLksoDCaxVBnH7rjvz2aXPU5A5QobHRV%2FivRb76heSTIKG3Wd%2ByUFHenIX4%2Bk2pSVl5R1Dp5DbgSbOk2%2Fxs9JF1KcqB3vzVZ1SG7%2FZ24MZIx%2BqvXsMeQU%3D). Edge-side methods ignore multi-tenant consent boundaries and operate without coordination across the federated topology, leaving other tiers holding stale model state.

Federated methods such as FedEraser, gradient-rollback aggregation, and federated SISA variants address the distributed topology directly [(Liu et al., 2021)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3A7VbLbmQ1EO0gXkJizw5rxAKkdCfpvJSREIKQoIGONBqNCCzddt17nfjaN7ZvOr1rFnzDiB%2FgH7Jhw4L%2F4DfYcMq3X5khYj1Sbzodu1x16tSpqv7lvvdu4YMpjfuAblqfaPr%2BH%2BekKchEWliSwRlXis%2FPR1%2BISkYRSJFLdiqoplDCBGdSNMHXJrKhNjEFM275dS1VZRytebmAl0YGqU1ZD8TzIFUySlrhiHQUvhCpIvEEcKokkhdjEgBX%2BpTIPRHSaaF86xKFHEkmBPYm%2BuxbpiTVdRRwZ%2FmVoKIwygCsSKQqZ25aivAvE0wc8qj9LW0LGLYuA9wWsSFl8EikIE12mmMUSC4Dy8fI63wkaq%2FJDsTZHdJlw7kP%2FroWzbj8Tnlgvkuc38VoW1R%2BQrcUtoUMJJwX1ruSAhsDMqmU0Z%2FDsEY4MK1bhPbZk3EVBU6JaTYO7Hm3iDKRU3bN6Jipi1HHewefExmIl%2Fwa3pE3W8IpOTm2tMZVzjiTg7Lkp4tsufaMKAorUXkAax100qe7xsKlHohnDMREVLjh9CaALa%2FxAXCFCTGJmKjhqIVBjbJpKRsxnkI%2FFBGd6YP4zoKM7GD1sFgqco3omlLldd9bX05XhSVrauNgOyessC05RUw%2BSrn0oyyn%2B%2Ffst9ilDB7ZvrR%2BjMQXOYtJZUBPNKVjYcisfOTaqlxp1oSpSbQRHrlqqDTk36Iu8%2Bs53AeiQRXEWEbozGhC5GKVswAlXGp0SIefmy0AEO4gmQw3Jh9kmTtjzX32ne9XGDgnwEMluO5rUdDECyNuiXWYXdooiWWNSsyFLhEUC3Fzs3IDg3v4aRtwB50jhdeZnbdaJW%2BRLBE3XOq6B6dvZgaR56Zethn%2Fg6miKGb356OB%2BBrNcgtwwGDGiMX5dRpg2oo24DEcAbf1DQKByIVpR%2BYSwhw3E2NUlftw8TyXEi95ojVc7SVT%2F0XUtpgasjrPn3WV8Cgh3W%2Bz2rvQD8oyn3Sv096pjZuMgS746O5yi0o15aGD9kJdmWT4KnzLFUWmGAkqqzkSrjSamEMusu%2BGi7klt%2BB01WkTk0ADmgeuFRdxiR92B3%2B%2BAgs1yo6LbAkiFzVaDseoEElVA3GJUO4W24DzBLSJD9d5S8C9DGA0TwEMLXRE8hMZMPnZvTWS%2BzQHsFRCGjzGUN8sOhUMz33JD9HH0oR8DRwuMjJwXkvnKAwoKoyjf77669Vns16vpzn%2Bfe%2FjSo6ZDJU2C26z4DYLbrPgNgtus%2BDeygVnpWLVkP4WmZfD3eFRf3e%2F5hnMxQ%2FPXOHve1ulbKGcEGfN8mbklYTGcWkaNNgLibF833sHJcUIeo6TrcrK7ttHll%2FlPQcfv8JKO%2FQE%2FhaFrI2dqpFpqWSyy%2B%2Bkn8KVDt5S703L8YXsDM1PRvobkPCoqf6ZIWXj%2FPVRu8uV3WmFj0fsViCvvue4qmrBaTZWRsf73ntKe3P36d7uYG9v92THTG58PBzungwHoHVvcHI43BseH2AXjd0Mn9HNVC3Lq%2F29k%2BP9o%2F2D44MKjeikpbvry9XhFSzbrjxX7Ke%2Fe9QfHpaxHadpw%2FCuZYCSLb3Ev1uQEfniB%2BP0%2BjE62dopbK2CLvQ301P%2BYfDJ7wn7jTekIn0J3cXu%2BEtKJgHFj0vJPxVnvPlYwWfL3XeaJ1d%2FxBNEAJ4ULx4swuWIGy1W0EVejEXyjVFxphn%2Fh6oN9m6nSqmJT3d2QOAAP2F2%2FofDfwE%3D). In a multi-tenant building, each edge device acts as a federated client; a consent revocation triggers rollback of that client's gradient contributions from the aggregated model [(Berkani et al., But federated unlearning weakens guarantee strength. The result is statistical, not certified. Verification difficulty compounds across clients: the least attestable node bottlenecks the global guarantee, and offline clients retain stale weights in their local models indefinitely [(Jin et al., 2023)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3ArVjNbxxJFXeyQWElxMeRBSlFFiFb8kzGY8exwyFenDUJeIMIdhI%2BdkVN9%2Bvpiru7mqrq8QwSrBdyQVy4IaQF8R8gwckHEEf%2BALQcOBBW4oCExK4WAcKLxO9V98x0TzbI1mwUJ%2B7pqlfv%2FT5ePfu144WL%2FSAusoPb4fDTHRl11mUQtTZW5FprbW11pbW5vrnektd6Gxuda9Gm3KB%2BogPptDleOPcwl326U6Q9MnIl0kb1VXYhzcPolurHCb6cxSrlNyid2R8fL5wPez09PF64EMXEC05u%2FHvrZwv4EyYUuZOt29%2B5%2BFs8BU7nJ1t7r%2F9qCw90qEIXn2zd%2FN4b%2FK526sJZA%2F73zjcbAf%2F0eL6A%2By%2F%2F9agZkB%2FnCfibR%2FwwDfiOmC%2FgvUuX%2BdU04OM5M7z36q85pVpA5miegI8fNFmel5T7W%2B80SXl8Ctlsvfjnt3hXGfDGuz%2F5OyulDHj97U%2F9vBbwY%2Flzp9HhW%2F95hpdVGd7743e5yjLgsxefrbF8483PRjMln3vvkn%2FKyFcBxcI9fioDfuIvraawfz9DylkDin8dNQPOYnjWgM%2B%2F%2B1WusRaQ458t4PMv%2FIF5KAN%2B5m%2Ffr2e4%2FcvX5sxw6e1vzGQ4J4bL%2F1yf0eHpSn69JuxGwCsnH5nJkFfOEXDl1X%2FUhf2k9c4acPW8en9LXnv0RrM5zBtw%2FeL2TD%2BcE8NrP%2FhFs2OfUjZPDbj5oU%2FWSen%2BsJgtmb5VaEejD1y6pQ9pQGZZuFhZkcqRUJkzOiwCElFhXExGWAoKo9xIGGUPrFARFpNw0vTJiSBRlDmBzQU2FtYdauwaCZmFItfWEv9F4EQFShfYneWFs23xlSKIq81WSENCJlYjRqIOKBkJp8VAJwgpjcJjbvRAhTgU1RvEVVZnFIpQOikwNvBJriWdk8EBgpLMWkXeFi9k2heQqFQ5P0AIHQkaKutU1hcpuViHdllYzkVasUPhi0Za7FjcVYVAdTJpL4tup7uy5AtSyHWAjCSSXuaaU0q1YcwoilAgZcGoLfZiAlaAWKEsi0GHD3NGYlUoUh1SYn3SJHEshqGeTATlOoiRiepnCpFwAKpWWWBIWt7OYUCfCFAojj6MFfYiAbI5BUomvDrNjQwcNid4T1l5FEJ%2BGwQwwgkz5uuQ5oEaXO%2BudtbbnW53ZX3QFV8PbHv38y%2BLbld8KXBc85rognoDdUzKtCWUFALbMFSMKB%2B9LLBIRSNfJ6QBPAPwzmDzI0glaTJ%2BCx79G3ysDflcPbtkvQ6CGM%2BU9ZFwWIBt7fcHOs0TsAYFImKmoZEMAUVIlIuMCoOCM2LhQZ2LN%2B%2FcsUttcTtDGmkqDbLjIBXZ4GWg6BBEqMx%2FjrMHXpi5NLLVNzKPgZbHiIQuHJ%2FlF%2B%2FJXkLitrCxPgQuqJiP1c5%2FPFUYpO3515EDBWMV%2BJOkQcEqnwgRAJSagBYabgLBQCz0cGqU0bMOggDLQvO3EDj7SOgcOZQciKn%2BIKLKr8ulCY0ayGBUogjlBuTBkRV%2FHh34bZRXZCTsoZRARaZsWio1IKQeqbHjpoTiCBGYghU4rjXldsCbDPWLhOd8NIMQNkRyOBrgWFA6JToeT%2FoepIxwCO%2BW%2BPZQ7OxD%2BSiS%2Bkgxlr7PQIroTSwIRt6nV%2FagkHLKwhKDMR7YkXHpaZGhPo88g1LZqUSoKcaqVqa1DXsFeHVy43cf7XFPDlljxwsfjiV4YLONLl5AQahvwEeBU7QQJgVF7I41v7izuyRitBcoDS2Nc4Al%2FbHiEA03RDsyqlfwJjuyjgA6wEdjR%2BJJIntI1akBJOz97BsJx0UPwBLvHTPgDwruXBXdbXG These methods assume a trusted aggregator and sufficiently synchronous clients, assumptions that break down in real building networks with intermittent connectivity and heterogeneous firmware.

The pattern across all three tiers is consistent: each approach addresses one or two of the constraints, from section 3, while ignoring the rest. Cloud-side methods ignore edge resources and federation. Edge-side methods ignore multi-tenancy and cross-tier coordination. Federated methods weaken the unlearning guarantee itself. No existing approach satisfies the full constraint envelope simultaneously. Compounding this gap, there is no shared benchmark against which to compare partial solutions. Each study uses its own metrics, datasets, and threat models. One measures membership inference accuracy, another activation distance, a third wall-clock time [(Carlini et al., 2022)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3ArVjPbyNJFfbuDpoLcESAOJSGoE2k2LEdZ%2BwsPybJzGQnOz92NJnVSMBIlLuru2vc3dVTVR3bI1YKHBD8ASvNAa32sBJIXFhxirggcUCIOxxAmmUXceAAXBBoI6H9XnU7sZ04OzueQ2K7u%2Bp7733ve1%2B1%2FYODyvnQi%2FK0t%2BMPFurdoNPuNFvVoLXaqbbaHVHtNrr1art10VvteEGTtzphrDxulT6ovPAg46G4lSddof3Gen09UFqGMj2XZH5wTYZRjD9rsFC6PVKl5vGXDyov%2Bt2uGhxUzgWRoBWHlz5a%2BdZGpVLxYxHYw42dN89%2FD588q7LDjUZ3ax8fRF%2F6NjrcuPLDv9G9scifPQ3wfxs%2FxapTAZv%2Ffp1iHQO%2BPydgS%2F%2F6%2FUnA38wHuPafRUIYA6SVcwC2%2B2%2FRh%2BeXYeejF9lz5fCV7%2Ffp1vMD%2FPr%2FPxxvyuafH7%2FzFIBn6PCb7%2F2JED41h2%2FPyvDSpb88U8kzATf4wjN1eSbg5oVvT%2BlwXsAP35qSzdMJeybg1rt%2FmCz5yZwZXg5emHSbeZty5eX2uA4bP9sngX2yDr9BEKcBXq28N%2Bk2f38awBMZ0qYS8He%2FmrIvYmAOwO0f%2F3NKh3MCvvrawuToPXkawBMcHgNe%2B%2Brbk4AfkCznyPDaP96ZMoc5S975xR%2Bf6UyZCfha8rmp0ZtTNtdrr05NypwZXv%2FXm5Mln%2BiyeJgrK4afebylbMS0MIJrLxLasO%2B0mvcZT32Wae5ZSU8a7vLF1fssN4IlggBMJDMm00Doqkg9wbi13OsZZhUWcJNrgf1yj3tDpgJmNZep8FmifBGbGruHDTrMBbMRt%2FiH8NhmtfQMM5HKY9%2BFMlarNDzCXjS5FzFumMq1WUJ0prQvNMXknpdrbkU8PBE%2BFryHumtsO7d0va90D%2BgSwNOlTFRS5uHhQUu6KJFgseqzgMdGVDNlQM2eYBQV%2FIUyEcuUSVdYi9V5ij3GEo8S9WPvGB1iT6TsQW4s4ywQfaoVDHs8xW4KGCBgalFLVwuOrvg19obhXRlLO2QyybTaEwlWlHTPLqLG7iJyT4AFmUjrHhspgYzaNuBJFp%2FsZ7m%2FWrAgEaPs0RBlPswlKEyRczQESMY1R98oe7cqFWiyI4Fo19TyQKvE1e9zy9H5SCKmtAScUDsTBUDUpDTIojiAGbWA41apgUXHGag06MGSE0GGHhThAg4qF7u5ZX00e2kZLyxSWSmvYKzvfRnHIwZdVp5KsrwghsdMBIH0JBhwbYLMylQYz7AFrVieVF1MgkAxRcm%2ByGI1REpY48oy0AJ0hjbsuJCkOYwbNo%2FEMKq0LzGFFA9sRgr9vimS6mxt4stBQWqXGxCJjyzhKZI%2BBbyExASvLrPWMmu079fY6y7UKY0vFrsyAZN6EhJZHs0CauRdtA%2Fl%2BtCBRxIdIxMTe6S7O8CRac9VjFiYTuDbsbFwdwJ2c2eT3CePbSlWaFxgpqhj0OqZExpBBTw1fVz2MUmpczYCxS58e3pUyP1hLoz7ulRj11QfMtLLBVmLYM2pKlHGLjmbcjWgbtzJOPRN1HqYTp9yIb5lyn0MAc184Ve0jQMTDlP1%2BBEDDFblCWOcPRii01jBAQNVFUhnW0mNCeNBQoeXfv%2FXX5J3%2ByTfg8rnI97FRMCYhy%2B1Ns%2BipxAnEcS4jwQN10NqHOjAG35syVA13hTz6kyRPJpWZqBVepipCL0kYjVLFVkWpt5KL485%2BCtNpI9WwLhsgQnxUIkO6WVYA4UiaLIAzIRr9LFY3JiXAoGgxB6PiWA6AmjTBLsXCqP3hheOzotixrl0OXsRHMmDguQjJFUmTrmUpJDHjhuse5UBCIEgSjZLJRy Without a canonical evaluation framework, claims of efficient or complete unlearning cannot be compared across papers, and no certification path exists for regulator-facing audits.

## 5. Open Challenges and Research Directions

Recent advances in adjacent fields provide building blocks that could address the obstacles already identified. Federated learning frameworks have matured, trusted execution environments are increasingly available on edge hardware, zero-knowledge proof toolchains have reached practical compilation targets, and TinyML runtimes now support on-device model manipulation [(Somvanshi et al., 2025)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3ApVjdbhxJFZ5dEi0XK65hJZYSQiJBMxPbiWMnEtoYW2HNxt4l4yhICEF11%2Bnp8lRXdaqqZ9y5IUhIIPECe4nEI6xA8g1wB%2BIVuOEZuGGlSIjvVE%2Fb4ySOgnKR2DVVder8fN93zvhXp4P3pnnZ2Nm%2BOvlOvr69VWxu5CO5vZ2NbhUZje5sZdnoTp7lGd1eW5M386lxuYzOnw7eOa7llA6bKiOf3VwrnNdTba9UtSo%2B1tPS4F8MOKbTDe1s%2BO3p4F2VZe7kdHClKIkPPP%2Fov5%2F%2F4dlgMFCGivj83rf%2F%2BeUvsMqjq5%2FfO%2FziWGBBC61i%2Bfze0cdf%2FgvLlWe%2F%2Fs3%2F0%2BKnv%2Fn3Xy9Y%2FA8v38biZ%2BPv%2F37V4v4VrN7O4ue%2FvnfB4lU%2B%2BTYWf%2Fzu33nRW%2FzgL5%2B9icV7G%2F%2BYsSO9xT%2Fe5EA7iw%2Ff59%2FPfVTvvW3U3x0dXrD48xv8wqrFD9%2FA4qqP3%2FulW436KHv%2FRfR8SE8aF6m9%2BqejkkReSmPITklYtxA6iOiEojkZVwup5tLmpETuqlqbBGfsO4NL2uJoKaOopY867UirRMhLUo0hcaRtu%2FdAVE6RCULm3oUgSork3ZQsuQYr6dVCehoKbSNNvYwkFjqWosZTIFYVhNEzmLr%2FQGPrQMNIemT3YLI%2FGR0eDtOqkie60k9J%2BMZGXSEkZws9bWCPvSoMnehMGx3bZN01UQT4owudazsVtfNRLvedFyQDCVf0OajIRvHTra2h2Nr%2B2Visb4hdZ3PTBGa2OCqRsND4ObWi9hRwGLGmdHkqyQY9J0EnstK2cwaGI5JOc2eazjvvqpSrgwec%2BS5rQ1H2UsIeligM30Jcc%2FLIXp48pIIjYP9SkoX0eYk85bGBJ0MRXBE5uysF6%2FLFaR%2BlnfM8lzIIsjIzKHZwNcKKGvKFlbMjpELjSS6SgVPpfW1FQIY8mVbgOdf4nEZwMESPl3CP7Fx7Zzl%2FYSwek8gIOimyFpk1OIF8IDQOK%2BSupuSZ4mdtnhKTUVwQ2T43vN0nh6oa%2FuqnvYGpdwv%2B3RLeRRIJMIcxoloYkt7yXi7rrsiac4MSAi6oFyqnBFIglIyoG5wlj32E6oRxi1HtFuTFwe4jlBUekAJPunR0QZUSBQaiOCK8DWd6UPfkSQlgiwCN6j3Gp%2Bd1tASoonx5ToYAWue7x0JNuZYGyFZiZ7K%2FCyuJcMzT0NSM22RMUW1cm4CacIuwLZLn%2FCx0dJqhF2VtpFHIJZhZUeV8i6hdrD0i7V6rNGq7kBGETjFnDWLl0k101ZgoLY3AWdMOxYIhjSf9MmJQicFihHewDhc6OLoaZNRPl6JBeWn1k4aYLnkpALcnjQRdu%2F0h2NNwnTqEHju4tWRRYKYJhhUkIhUPdxcAIv9csiJ6nSU6BX7%2BcGfC8IwSYSO%2B6UViJIBwGc%2FAykF%2BivMMxZ4zQSvIEiIFCaSBYik4hrDjktNesmKmdJ8lv%2FCyolHK%2B3CpmMjji%2BzbaaIDns%2Bpl2oKT0hWjKIOH1aNohvhR6%2BjRheUt7mhsSAQxtPzj%2F725zm3ccUvng6%2BVsqM48lje%2FULFnYva60SNwABlosV7Ca%2BK49IurgVVexbIkIKqEtbTBqJyl6g%2FrWd%2FesCPidHYXQopnrOF7yGdC5FTBxIJB3hPOgZeK1jMq7iJQ3UsQime7mDcJPnRjLsLu8xiC%2Fe3HtwHYwrkdNeErQF7tC8GMp5E5JWdSLGl4KuapzVtoBIsdtRBhACRyruIwk1Dp2P6f4SJfvmJZlDklucVHpaiVDqAoE7YIQFps%2FURalZtjxG7WsVsleK8Zu1EQdbc02Lvof None of these were designed for unlearning, but each can be adapted. The following subsections identify concrete research directions organized around three axes: scalability, verification, and the regulatory-standardisation gap.

### 5.1 Scalability and Efficiency in Large-Scale IoT

Several research directions could bring unlearning capability to resource-constrained tiers. Sparse and structured update primitives restrict unlearning to a small fraction of model parameters, analogous to LoRA-style adapters, reducing memory requirements enough to fit within MCU constraints [(Liu et al., 2023)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3ArVhNjBxHFV4HB%2BcEggOKIyEVEgo2bM%2Fu7Hg9u1FEvF6v4012%2FbdrOdiySE1X9XTtVne1q6rnJwjkREKIAxc4cIuQzAGEuCDlsJwiJK5wRMolIZE4ckFIyJYQ36vumZ1Z7ULE5GDPdk%2F1%2B%2Fne9773et4%2BmDvTjdMy398Ug6%2BvrEi%2BlMRx1Fxe5NGF1kUerS7zJFqVF1f4ymq8IqXoahNzb%2BzB3Km9gnfl9TLrSBu3VtuJsaqr8tNZIZJrqptq%2FPMO51R4RJnc%2Ffxg7hnR6ZjBwdzpJJV04OmlZ%2F75YzY3Nye0THC1%2Bf0z7%2BMq9qZ4eunVd%2F%2F8ES5kXwmfPr105Z1P6OSE3%2BeOMfjKvy69i1MTBt8cG7z25ufJwqHBj8jZDAY3v%2FYyXRwa%2FJACnsXgx%2FbRdISXZjP42uNfUY6fncHXxYfTGD6Z0eDWi2cnU163u3Tyfxl8shCQrw2%2Btt0hG7XBf6STEa7%2F7mczRrj9%2Bz2yMFFlcjaDwevf%2B%2BUvPlPa3Hj5gyNVnjHCG%2F%2F%2BEn11aPBv%2F1frUZaVwZvffTyd8l8%2FTZX%2Fm8H3vz0tDnQ1i8Fb7IPplP8yI21uvd2f7pRZq3zr4y9Pq82svXz78m%2BnDf59VoOPrx0h9ow83PncJ5PEvvDT9z6NYj9ZWKFEjhOHnb2fTIvDDyjgWSL80xeOzJQZU95t%2FPpIlY%2BmLB%2BWxsvhs2e2VM6kZ1w32P3mxdUHzMpC81gKFpu8xzpDxlmmYmtYVmqvIs2H0rICA1sW3ho8DHtC4Hxf%2BbQ6VGjJXMa1ZvvS5lJXps5t64L%2BON9guwZuuFZvSSaTRMVK5p5d2WIqT6SVeSwZDG%2BT13WTw4vW8HknVx5G1u%2B48%2FOVs6WoxYwV0jpmEpbxLk6UovaNJzKZGTtkPuU5y0xHIawiNbl082wi7cKawjgkANPXce9%2Bc%2BnBPNsQXW5HRzql0p794b3razvsfrv5gHnDXCFjVeWQGYEkuY1T5WXsSysdwy5DBh3jpTcZlpcYMQ0bTDTYTW55Jr20Czz2qhcWG%2Faw5LlXb4WLBrs1cYWA2ouICB%2FwbCUgchQACgVHQE7lXdZaijqAJ9EGz%2BTdqDAKkFaBFeQvIoeuws1KjVM9qYdMmz7ugSXAROWxLgVZ6weKkUeCgqKM6jB7XJeSvmg9QBnTUe7BReUhBtgdycpcJWrEikCaqK%2BcJIcUKS7IKTsnG93GPGtepJvzbKX6CMngbvUhfdw4X2UsSnCjLmvpQGfmFKoOCgEvPZynqsBpWYByWVESFIg%2FF6NHC9MHL2LjfCAhF7zwZNkjkx63Q0q%2BNk9PVUZC5lwzrTJVXQCsbZ6XEoQa9Q6QqrCAe7I2xeau5UVKvVSAG7jvZRdhGHRRhWrpgmM1wMNTAB2hxZ0cZK%2BYh5TZ7bXtEObVrbWda6O40WLOWw4nDp4CB4%2BJtvUATiVgQZAGvZxRB2rJbU6RAJFCxfshkY50PlTT2GwqHIYo6yoS2SsiVMwB6xHWIbtdg11RLi6dC2ms6S42fZ9mkZZgIYDwgLa2ig5Kc%2FWwRA%2BlvIcCp6afA0HJPbVqRiRCXqGgFOtEjYLXGgYLhVM25FWz3oXXCXBeyBjWqON5HJeWx2hL6WJj5dNX%2Frj3lfAq0Td2%2F2DuiynvEJixH57pE9thz%2BINpcvAbS1Idbj1VAsoQairhpNKwBKgh5Og%2BNqm2Z1na5vfwud5phwTFs0H%2FEiaJLWfdGhlDt4iNzx4aMgTiSxHDGWtK5QiPaUy6n4HQ8yVcYw%2F6UkhZXFYxnNXtiC2dyl5ekRA2c0wVBpHQdDQz1aZkphysssE0JeWDFoVA0eZO4NGp0j6k q8EmwOfR4EzPuT6ICIny%2FFgFFLIm%2Fo47%2FAA%3D%3D). A two-tier approach offers a pragmatic middle ground: the cloud performs heavy retraining while pushing a lightweight residual patch to edge devices, bounding the computational cost at the edge without leaving replicas permanently stale.

These directions remain largely unvalidated. No benchmarks exist for unlearning time or energy consumption on Cortex-M-class targets. Formal bounds on residual error when sparse updates replace full retraining have not been established. Compiler support for unlearning operations in TinyML stacks does not yet exist. Until these gaps are filled, scalable on-device unlearning remains a promising direction rather than a deployable capability.

### 5.2 Verification and Formal Guarantees of Unlearning

Three privacy-enhancing technologies offer partial pathways toward auditable erasure. Trusted execution environments such as Intel SGX, ARM TrustZone, and AMD SEV can run the unlearning procedure inside a hardware enclave, producing a cryptographic attestation report as evidence that the declared procedure executed on the stated inputs [(Weng et al., 2024)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3AvVlLbBxJGXYgvCQkDiC0uRVhEbHwjOdhO3Y2u5uHnY2zcfCuE7whirQ13dU9FVdXdbqq57Enswc4ceCCQCwIDnviwDk3JEBCvCQOcAAE0QouoL2jtUB8f3X3POx4CTtLJHs83V31P7%2F%2F%2B%2F9qf%2BXB3EfioJvrvc1w8HRrpRlFfHWt1gxXOrWltUZQ6yx32rXVpeZaY7mFX96IlQm4M9mDuRP3Uh6LG3nSEVnYbjdbkclkLPXJJA2jqzLuKvw6i4XS75FG22899WDuA2GnYwYP5k5GXUErDp5%2FZ%2FH8q3Nzc6ESkTu4wOZ2Ga4CZ9KDCxd%2F8dcf40L0Zei6BxfWX%2F%2F9Pi4nNH%2FwUQL%2FeeF7WPVIgZe%2B8Q96NBb4L7r8nwVeGAm8vPMZuhgL%2FPOMFq5%2FapckjAU%2BpGezCPzd16dj%2BOv35PJY4MY3fzodw4cUgRkEXtn997TLs2b5hVNL35%2B2kAA2i8A%2F7B1yecakXP3OD%2BhiQiAZ%2FN8EvkulbN79LD0aC%2FzTjDG89vTnppPyl4czCvzbnWkcPl4MvzwgdD1K4Itv%2FmS69H4zY1Kuxz%2BfTsqsLm%2BdOQTs374nC8dss%2FX3VTJpIoYz0teNH5pDMaQczeDyF9Wb7y9stj%2F99iRsLp%2B68zi1%2FM7i6nGw2e7%2F6FAtP07pvZuFf2y8v3z40jO%2FnE7KrPT10hvRNDkcsVDcz40Tww99bVM7odjOC6%2BwDR0o3hNsfIcrZfqWBZlAO9cx48yKIM8ES0RSM9mQZSJGm19gmiciZNwyUchYYM4wMcBiJxhPUyWLgYAFJhSW9aXr4quOZCi0k1xJN2Rch0xCdZzRVZzzjONK2AUmrVHcQUGUmYS5rmAmdxZ7WYr7GEMS1u%2FKACK5Zh3Bcu2y3GJDnW06OGENSzPTwwYLFzKRwHPGnRPWFVYlIuhyLW3COkNos3nprIw1d%2FC31uEW6iHEROzMhsbiANfbmezxYMg218dLmQ26IhHsTnP57jyDbFJ4P4cqbPAxXyDPHZealJAzieAWOxOEgkE%2B3SrD%2BNb%2BtxF9hMwHJ%2BSO19kuxY7WeGsWxg4FSpIEigG3VmTOr%2BI5PhHjgII6LZ0V0S9DTw8QNteNcqWFtbTYqy7S6NPMIycyJh2zeccGmewgoEg0bfWg8fZenAjsjsh6MhB1dgVJ4opdyXVAD4qMb0G8oijsdCUTlKk6u7O8epdRSrHkNVgE0dHUJpgFDwmdI6xJ3TOqR4JGnmnpYfUarxWWVw9MKrJiNPVOT4BgIrJTChfYzYwnr8LvysKzK3cJqJkJ84AyUwmviSQ1fZEh0W%2FtvxGYJJGuBi01Ah9y%2Bd1DnpT1ERLouEcoKbmfyzTFTV8jHCnh2qY8o9RWTnjLGVbLSIqszm761B1dJwk8mgqFbPICxwUGfYgF%2BdfJXfEwQdgoS9hUmy5NlESS%2BrAhlPW4vsCgLkQxas8mLBaa4mqyIqwURKoISsmegAptHFQHKg8r0EN9jLhaluXar5N6Ept1gAPFFAoUioLxKF3gzZsAvEcCigC8i4iUDuWAXawT7Hiwh1gQWQG9BUYmOa3wuyeJTEgOko8dfilpnoKUzUEn4DMgcQ%2FWwV8BrgnKr9X5xjubGaU6pLqS15dKEQ0h%2FTL2vIUEmzwjJxAUy85YAXUi8NhkX6pdmq8z%2BAeHD57%2F2a9CT%2Bp9KH4w94ku71ikNnDDj32B0nz6ZeJ%2B8h0KUFSxAYL1aURREepZwoOu1IIpwTMf1jNb1%2BcRbEhAHcI%2F3lEoWmtAURKhAynmyDhRSykUJSlGXOvxB3khS6hWwQCWpXlm88KpTAQAiRpiUw9BIdhWBuR6ZIIDu2pJHAiogi1g6xAYS1L88 A hybrid approach combining TEE attestation for runtime evidence, zero-knowledge proofs for post-hoc audit, and differential privacy accounting for cumulative guarantees could provide layered verification across the building stack.

Each pathway faces its own barriers. Zero-knowledge proof generation for million-parameter models remains prohibitively expensive. TEE memory limits are incompatible with full-model unlearning and require enclave-friendly model partitioning schemes that do not yet exist. Most critically, no legal precedent maps any of these technical mechanisms to GDPR Article 17 compliance, leaving a gap between what can be cryptographically proven and what regulators will accept as erasure.

### 5.3 Regulatory and Ethical Considerations

Compliance-by-design pipelines could bridge the gap between legal obligations and technical mechanisms. Treating the data-subject lifecycle, such as consent grant, update, revocation, as a first-class state machine wired directly to unlearning triggers would automate erasure rather than relying on manual intervention [(Sha et al., 2024)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3ArVdNbCTFFZ4lG4XkkENyiJJTZZVDInlmZ8Yer40UsbtEG2%2FYJUQLWiSElOrq191lV1c1VdVjTy4xyiUXoiQHJISQkDgicfcJTkgckLhzQRwQR7QSSGCQ%2BF51%2B2d2lz%2BZgz3umlev3vu%2B733Vfu5g8JNSVa3duZ7v%2FU5t0nqxUWwMN3Ophmvj9WwoN1YvDS9NSG4Weaay1Y3SOCWj8weDc9uNLOmxts7IZ9O1wnldanu%2BbvJiS5eVwU8MCNNph3Y2PH8weCDPMrd3MDhfVMQBhw9%2F9uDz7w8Gg9xQEQ8vTwc%2FexNPKrrm8PK1Nz7cxwPt6jxWh5f%2F9s9PBR5PHfur75nwzy9vXV5K%2BDmezpJw6x%2Bvc0k%2FXIXXn%2Folf3WS8JMzJvzLrH1lKeEXjMAZEj76i%2Ff%2BvpTwS%2BboLAk%2F%2FuMyKXfOmPDGO68uk%2FLJGVu%2B%2BdpPlxI%2Bd8CRZ0j42L%2B3%2BeFUy%2Fx4hoR%2FpXeXWb7z3YT9fw67X8LHf%2FvSEin%2FyZn0b0%2F4wqmEkzsfcY4%2B4X9v8FfHCf%2F35t0t07Oti7T48dtPVDqIxjtFIQiyMjPEz6R0IKFzslEXurMUIW0uPNVuLo1whchllCuicCGS17YU0bchClk7%2FN0G8kFkCyxKGxrpkcgsRKjcbgqtZMQv0j5lEUpakZFQrm4MRUIkeRkoF22Dcz2qpRBH4kqea65EGrNY6XYabQltCXShfKs0SoM54ljXciuVc5EP7EpHnzliMm10RIKKTJOqcQKfjdM2Ik%2FAWQIpjsJJuDaiMqzuVmRF7XIyQaAlhKA9FJCLXR0rhAlFPmIl1RYoBvH0ZHVFTKb4NV1fe2YkttyuqKWqsEu01pD0lkvoAZBNYzTSIYNydru1KgHP2ZfbraXFRw1URViAgDowAuAtYKlL8DQfKGRIoFteuabLFlVPJiNxlXB9pJNTblCRKPtg%2F0UkQgYvu5PnWgopSi%2BbCiow3XeFVLRyvCfVLlUSEMPcQFhQDXN0xFyKPaWc0EBfkFXqaSSSBlN7iLOAJSdfuk6HvTS50ozALOGDH8AX4EY5LiU%2FgvQY0MQSp4YQykQNIUQAqgaa9a7uDpTq2VaHJCqWQdrFmtUpSaea0JooSrIAheNS56ijE4ByPu%2F6bhtkRNE1NrMGwAIyKtlEBj2dVhiwryrJ6GJmQtSq24s1W1LooagJX7TGtaE%2F4IgncMjNfrMSBJB3uwCU5mRcw3OIOio5J%2BbRtkaCMmcxIeBiDv4Sftx4Yv8YwR75kbjNpMiO657Qnq6e0k4L%2FemsPxwJTVw40fcF0VSYhxUh822YRGcBX8dap6JuVJkEYWmXZ5AJ79jocWK78ITqgkZzCVz8DbTRMRuMtAuAwWJOb0QrAj6UkJUe2mQwyWNweis76aZjtjAEltIoJdDZEpFmWMsdLuvYFLD9GMFkUTg%2F6ZVXM422%2B3JqiXLAOnK1nWEiAJIuKSZEesBX4Bmz8TPH%2FOVAoIsqWmN5ykKUaifZXKp71%2FmdkaAApdDhw2%2B9ts5Xes6rB4OfVzIL0LOKiwfKBBrwmRN3PwePukySTfUDV82TzT1bmUSL1k7VByDuJgwm7GVydA0S%2BB20my1LrXdB6f76YNqwA%2FOXgGgcJg6RJ6nhUmwiOYYlUXlkfGAOs8a7WIE5FaBkBSTjOWmDC79bsL18gB4OZ6Kg8UKnc3rd9BAUTkHTILAjOMOEIy4Rero0ZOPVZM39uzVsA%2B0FMQedPKX36Di02bDQZGANqWO2SL5thIZiUVAvdAwnTsIE45Lhc7rG%2BgFvvJ5LtRiJm%2BCVm%2BjGrJHYJXIdUDxXr1rPlytbiDHELgJ%2FazvP0bCPXvopPRyETRzTH controllers [(Merabet et al., Cross-controller protocols would standardize how revocations propagate from building owner to vendor cloud to edge gateway, resolving the controller ambiguity that currently leaves unlearning responsibility undefined.

On the standardization side, intelligent building stacks span vendor silos with proprietary BMS protocols, and no shared substrate exists for cross-system unlearning. A shared benchmark combining multi-modal sensor data with multi-tenant structure, paired with a standardised metric suite covering completeness, utility retention, energy cost, and communication overhead, would make claims comparable across studies [(Liu et al., 2025)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3ArVnfix1JFZ5oMPu0LyLiErAEhQTn3ty5mUkmi5BM7uTH5Mfm12azMYS1bld1d2WqqzpV3XfmZhGiT4KvggrLovgi%2BOCrA6KC%2F4GC%2BiIbBPEvEFby4nequ%2B%2Fte5NZWO5dNpl0ddWpc77zne%2BcTn5wsHIsidLS7O6I%2FW%2BeWx%2BeEWv9zc6Zs3y9sy42ep3N%2BPTpztlNLqIhl4JH%2FUTbiBfWHawceZLzRL5TZkPphv1ebJ1KlDma5SK%2BqpJU41fhsU2FE8oa%2F7PjBytfEMOh3T9YORqnkna8PP%2FphY9X8J%2FQMi5eXtj5%2FrE%2F4SkqbP7ywtZf%2F0iv5J4SRfrywvYPP3mOx9a9X%2F6cBi%2F%2B5L%2FfmzH4gt4tYHDw3rfJpeUZ3P5qPBvyiwVD3v7HRxeWavDSz%2F8yFzLZX8Dg5Udv%2FGKpHl45fn4OwwU9vPLPchbDT14sZvDqx7%2BdC3lBD3c%2B%2BDc9LK9Srn3jOFuuwX%2FdnSu9BUO%2B%2FqsfzfJw0aTcEH8gC8szePNb%2F1subW7%2B59RcyAsm5Z1fy7mQFzR4S300p9gLhnz7K2q5tXx7dHRODxc1%2BLdfLlcP75z9znLF4c5P%2F77cLN%2F51C83y3cfvjmrNou20bsHv1lu6d1763I7yyd%2B%2F7t5DOXT0hZy%2FKUn942KlRTMF9wI7oR6FsYfFmnu6I1nRSrx1kmTFKln2MX2JN810nu8tDEbYaMt8cZFqSpkVJRO%2BlVm80JljTUspx2jnpb0hkxwnWAEK9IMz9LwoVYmYTgnyYp0uNWyYam0YGWO83Jf%2BYK27Fm3WxmIIqml44UMDgo5ktrmmTQF%2BaSMsSNcPYLrtqPLMNF12WXrYIpnuZar7JJIJNvauShNlLJH%2FdNnHuNkTHcPaSnjbtezzAoZfCvGuYq4ZpJO%2BQg%2BI2rPtNqVbGdwn%2BW4jS7PrFGYOOlIcLMsrLEZ4TOSqYo0ARDbqPS0A5EJXnAmEJ1Tw%2BBmhTDC1JYLFlmt%2BdAizvAqctZ75CZchZhVRPYIAPKrgpaeIm1LgXscDGS5k6k0nsCQRnQK28EPlkuH9xk3EZZHXJfhii67h6wh93pcITSB5%2Bxjljs7UgJZ58HBWNu9zpB7sCcg1iHIQuA5dvIohT8ae2gJARd2QgdkqDHAtE1UtFrhgD0OHJ3GMQNM%2BxTompAjNSAIEnsoTaomT8Exyrt2KrHocxmB1BF5qJHOYHaSzC7bIsdzp4hVkxR48CwKOxUu1N6yyJWRAhce9XuPAVhtFLECs%2BaYZz61JfjrZEzn8ZPrDt5iqX17lHLHo0I6IniEZCoT6VJQLAESZQrKXQHTVZThGC7HUwc83g%2BvCC8sZKVp7IKPMGpgMOcOlsuQU5ibYoq8kXWNjx%2Bg1Gb1HkoQ4NP9ZDkUwUwucCN24Sj9nL3YjqRLJXAj9lUnC8eVaSpCmQ6KjC6EEdwSQQZCZcWAwTpk4Xoqb%2BWeOHfuMYtLByKgbLM85V49Q9KdRJYEUjBUGtFTTMR%2BInbFe7kPcivSAtTyuKZtuL9dT6EcRspZE1RjD2oEB1uH6SIL6K0jhJmBYA6t3W2fqsWMZaUuFNLBC6oplskitcKC2jiJ6zNbkdNx45EOBD8OB%2FERCpDg%2F2HF%2BC5qgI9AZV7HihpolDrkx0vE2FaMmqIAU%2BmOAltqBZ8TSCP3XlXoSqAB%2FHr%2Fcat252S59i%2ForkKKQ6zoBIAOrkHJfdooQlV4j%2Fr9s6gT6SPr5Mvzf%2F7wGrVPQR4frLyZ8iF4hdyPj31IglPncDZR0wI%2FcWkwGJxk4AIoIV2Cq%2FBHznI1sgVYBHi5UEkW6MeFgPNBaoMqzhSQkMCbH Integration with existing building protocols through BACnet, KNX, or Haystack tag extensions for consent and unlearn-state fields would embed erasure capability into infrastructure that buildings already run [(Mishra et al., 2020)](https://dummy-citation.com/citation?d=z%3ArVbPbyRHFfai8POAkLgkEUglxGFXco97xp4ZmwPx2J7dLLG9aL3OXqnpet1dnuqupqp6foQDBg75A7iAxIEzZw74lisgThxy4YDyL%2BQEvuSr6h7PRJETRRtLHqmrXr33ve9971X95mbrm1mS1%2BX0qVj8WHSTeEiCR4MJT6K9wUES8UHcj%2Fq9frI74HG%2F2%2B9mSifcaXOz9eCq4hmd18WETNqNd%2Fd6w1QbmcnytaIS6dsyyxX%2BnYWpDKekLu37N1tfE5OJXtxsvZbm5A1u3%2Fr%2Fh7%2Fawp9QlLrbwx9GEcNX4nR1e7j36zd%2BgQ%2BaS%2BHy28NL%2B0%2F%2FuRH6e1%2FSYf%2Fj2Z83HZq%2FX7%2Baw2H2702Ewx%2F97fCLHR5Gv%2FvLfQ4P%2FhN%2FKuW68nuvgPDkr%2FTfDYfb3%2F%2Bpt3wFh%2BN%2F%2Fcnn%2BNUhfPz%2BPz74VFE%2B9DXadEi%2FrLWj5Te%2B85IYL51MZMUdMZdzx9LauJwMk0Vl9IwKKp1lTJbsbb60jidTnBBMB5uj0QUryHHBHWfYLAU3wrK5VIpRyScK7lmhDbHKUCItbbMJOUcm4rXTBWIKxqtKyUbPTKfM1Iq8B5cjJIKwyxFzmrEEcneGt4uOZ2iNjOnaVbVjtuIJddiobGABeCGt309rVyO6IUvcJDkTEjhCKGnhZOptOPvo%2Bg8gLhJ6Xn50%2FUePyGgO6zmcATo6UpvCm4I3WXlGAgcWfFDBpMCCTFc5TAjWa4SyFHImRc0Vq7T0XAZ2clIV9rzjkFDYC0CWFY55GACRkLWMW6arSltwBSIm1GC2wOLjwi9QVE3tkBQtKKk9r4ynIHrtHI5aWNisAzthz%2BjG9%2BUIBAohfRJcqSVKVUslvN0MxyEe9lAbVNM68Jl4Egw5o1PpVgaPGlociLAQFaBhgaulBS6UFopSQU%2BIVlCS8zLYNDRaNlmuI4IFYgkvGVfW58xSDm0bHEQkDeDweOcaBPG28oov8YtYvDIS85OxaannigTQN8ylDY2NsjrsQhZScdCkWa7n7FgXBZnEs3rUYrFsXJLJltgrbV1UocgXtZnRkj08Phof20csB4SmmN47XDNBM1K60Qrg%2BLVVnp6jtZBwaFUHp0PaKxLsdtgIvcUnULrXYyP%2FO57mCOw%2FGkGGzvAuEHFt48E1KnCtYJCs720Il1mZlUG7gNJk0HYiAkGJm%2FXxSDXSL%2BR7K%2FEI39Qwj0ID3uVydorCqwz8u7zwaYBcr3eCNCZK2jxYGVLNLZbLKuhjQ6rt15qmtu83uNlIOMBcidxajfp5jfkhwjZ71qH%2F0xQ9jY%2FW3PuVhZ8dx%2B%2BcP3t5Oj55cjY%2Bf8FeeIXNtZkGijGt8qA%2BaNTHPA%2FAEfQ5lTQPc64VySkqZfyVDnGcPx%2BfPtoGZ4SV5vBIKcnLlqqLGnzIZkw2x7fZ6enx9h2Pl52LDjuhihu3ElIb5uHJs%2FEjVpcCaocuMRgTx851J%2FyfjKPR8e4givefPOvt78b7PstEK9Vg8%2BUN1Bz5cYprhY2edlCbBBnevvXB%2F77t70vhc7%2FZ%2Bq7iiR9CJE6QQtaLe%2F2o2y0wjD1yMk8hGdxKWUOQvVbocUB53HRrt9cZDMlJp2jx5qiZ%2BGH2lqGZnKnDLE5nWtUFvb6o7vye3r2LcDNl9JyXGeEWhFKNdT%2FHyptbD37%2Fca74xpfyp42cII5pXkYlL%2FypNOVo8mV6BuUZTpmcUZle8CmEJ4xWtPVZW3qi%2BNy2pqNSGJrfZ5oe18jPNLZ0slQQ5n1eHxteThvL7AJTG%2F18n60YvYfvYJqf1TkvCi7us5VnujGQLeLH3OD0vTDepbKkxvTqZ8TLCFNQt0gSKfDG%2FHoitFz8oBt3unF3sHPVQYFBbwcC8Eu7e92ukHZSXuPXlr%2BV8UFvEPX3477s7g970XD%2FoJsUPLuC%2BPaGu8Nufy9HG6BjaDF9uV68wum60daVdx3FB1G3X1R1mBIreT0I%2BKdjZWkm%2Favj3SkudF2bhC6NWrDcucr%2BZGeHm4WcdbTJdvBi3unF8W4nHg4O4szWEz8WwcAUrSQTRS%2Fw%2BQDSI52%2Bgxt6cxnPGdx%2BsFWJRNceLY91XbrX33CGwuhISLxEb9hmebeV94tLP0b9cwbPDJxLwrvDN7Kf35Ewnuz1W%2Bnu%2FZKu53T7JPIN0o7cFK8Smdhr4dF%2FK6mR7PYqWdQnpPp5JfoE).

The obstacles are not purely technical. No legal-technical mapping exists to define which metric and threshold pair satisfies erasure under GDPR Article 17. Fairness audits of post-unlearn models are virtually absent from the literature, despite the risk that forgetting one occupant's data degrades service for others. No vendor consortium has adopted a reference consent ontology, and no conformance test suite exists to hold implementations accountable. The regulatory and standardization gaps reinforce each other: without agreed standards, regulators cannot specify technical requirements, and without regulatory clarity, vendors have no incentive to standardize.

These three directions are interdependent. Scalable on-device unlearning without verification produces erasure that cannot be audited. Verification without standardization remains vendor-local and incomparable. Regulatory compliance without scalability is unenforceable at building scale. Advancing any single axis in isolation is insufficient; the research agenda must progress jointly, requiring collaboration across systems engineering, machine learning, and law.

## 6. Conclusion

Machine learning models deployed in intelligent buildings retain statistical traces of occupant data long after raw records are deleted. The regulatory obligation to erase this influence exists under GDPR Article 17, but existing methods face significant challenges in fulfilling it under the constraints that intelligent building IoT systems impose.

Exact and approximate unlearning methods each carry fundamental trade-offs, and three competing formal definitions reflect a field that has not converged on what erasure means. These methods, designed for centralized static settings, break down against the three-tier architecture, multi-tenant consent regimes, and fragmented training pipelines of intelligent buildings. Cloud-side methods ignore edge limitations, edge-side methods lack cross-tier coordination, and federated methods weaken the erasure guarantee itself. The absence of shared benchmarks prevents even partial solutions from being systematically compared.

This work has limitations. It does not follow a systematic approach, and the inclusion of preprint repositories means some referenced work has not undergone peer review. Intelligent building-specific deployments of machine unlearning remain rare, so several operational scenarios discussed generalize from federated learning or generic IoT settings rather than reporting direct experiments. 

Closing the gap between unlearning theory and building-scale deployment requires progress on three interdependent fronts: scalable erasure mechanisms that operate within edge resource budgets, verifiable guarantees that satisfy both formal definitions and regulatory expectations, and standardisation frameworks that make methods comparable and interoperable across vendor boundaries. None of these can succeed in isolation, and all demand collaboration across systems engineering, machine learning, and legal disciplines.
